# List 26

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>topsyturvy</b></del></td><td>/ˌtɑːpsiˈtɜːrvi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>混乱的</td><td>a topsyturvy world<br>颠倒混乱的世界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Since the move, our daily routine has been completely topsyturvy.  搬家以来，我们的日常作息完全乱了套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disordered</td><td>/dɪˈsɔːrdərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混乱的，无秩序的</td><td>a disordered pile of papers<br>杂乱的一堆文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disordered shelves made finding anything impossible.  杂乱的书架让人无法找到任何东西。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chaotic</td><td>/keɪˈɑːtɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>混乱的，杂乱无章的</td><td>a chaotic desk<br>杂乱的书桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The office looked chaotic after the sudden move.  突然搬迁后办公室看上去一片混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 messy</td><td>/ˈmesi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>凌乱的，脏乱的</td><td>a messy bedroom<br>凌乱的卧室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His room was so messy that the floor disappeared.  他房间乱得连地板都看不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anarchic</td><td>/æˈnɑːrkɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无秩序的，混乱的</td><td>an anarchic classroom<br>混乱失控的课堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The substitute teacher faced an anarchic classroom by noon.  到中午时，那位代课老师面对的是一个乱成一团的课堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Jumbled</td><td>/ˈdʒʌmbəld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>混乱的，杂乱的</td><td>a jumbled pile of clothes<br>杂乱的一堆衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His notes were so jumbled that nobody could follow them.  他的笔记杂乱得没人看得懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kempt</td><td>/kɛmpt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>整洁的，梳理整齐的</td><td>a kempt appearance<br>整洁的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived kempt and calm despite the long flight.  尽管长途飞行，他到达时仍整洁镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 neat</td><td>/ˈniːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>整洁的，井然有序的</td><td>neat handwriting<br>整洁的字迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her neat handwriting made the notes easy to read.  她整洁的字迹让笔记很好读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 orderly</td><td>/ˈɔːrdərli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有秩序的，整齐的</td><td>an orderly queue<br>整齐的队伍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students left the hall in an orderly line.  学生们排成整齐的队伍离开礼堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 organized</td><td>/ˈɔːrɡəˌnaɪzd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有条理的，有组织的</td><td>highly organized files<br>条理分明的文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She keeps an organized notebook for every course she takes.  她为每门课都备有一本条理分明的笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shipshape</td><td>/ˈʃɪpʃeɪp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>井然有序的</td><td>keep the office shipshape<br>让办公室井然有序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor kept his tiny cabin perfectly shipshape at all times.  老水手始终把他的小舱室保持得井然有序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tidy</td><td>/ˈtaɪdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>整洁的；整理</td><td>tidy up the room<br>把房间整理干净</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please tidy up the room before the guests arrive.  请在客人到来前把房间整理干净。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trim</td><td>/ˈtrɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>修剪；削减</td><td>trim the hedge<br>修剪树篱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He trims the hedge every June before the neighbors complain.  他每年六月在邻居抱怨前修剪树篱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncluttered</td><td>/ʌnˈklʌtərd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>整齐不杂乱的</td><td>an uncluttered desk<br>不杂乱的书桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She works best at an uncluttered table.  她在不杂乱的桌前工作效率最高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-ordered</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈɔrdərd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秩序井然的，有条理的</td><td>a well-ordered household<br>井井有条的家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She kept a well-ordered household despite four small children.  尽管有四个小孩，她仍把家管理得井井有条。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>torpid</b></td><td>/ˈtɔːrpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的，无生气的</td><td>a torpid winter afternoon<br>慵懒迟滞的冬日下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left the whole village torpid and unwilling to move.  酷热让整个村子都变得麻木迟钝，不愿动弹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 asleep</td><td>/əˈsliːp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>睡着的；麻木的</td><td>my foot is asleep<br>我的脚麻了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His right foot was asleep after sitting cross-legged so long.  盘腿坐了那么久，他的右脚麻了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 benumbed</del></td><td>/bɪˈnʌmd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>麻木的，失去感觉的</td><td>benumbed fingers<br>冻麻的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His benumbed fingers could not tie the frozen rope.  他冻麻的手指系不上结冰的绳子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dead</td><td>/ˈded/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>死的；毫无生气的</td><td>a dead town at night<br>夜里死寂的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The resort is completely dead in the winter months.  这个度假地在冬季几个月里毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensitive</td><td>/ˌɪnˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感觉迟钝的，无感觉的</td><td>insensitive to pain<br>对疼痛无感觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scarred skin was completely insensitive to heat or pain.  那块疤痕皮肤对热和疼痛完全没有感觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numbed</td><td>/ˈnʌmd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>麻木的，失去知觉的</td><td>numbed by the cold<br>被冻得麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Numbed by the cold, she dropped the keys twice.  被冻得麻木，她两次弄掉了钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfeeling</td><td>/ənˈfiːlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷酷的</td><td>an unfeeling response<br>冷漠的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed unfeeling to laugh at such a moment.  在这样的时刻发笑显得毫无感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 feeling</td><td>/ˈfiːlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有感觉的，敏感的</td><td>a feeling heart<br>有感情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a feeling reader would notice the quiet grief in that chapter.  只有有感受力的读者才会察觉那一章里静默的悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>torpor</b></td><td>/ˈtɔːrpər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣；（肉体或精神上的）迟钝，懒散</td><td>sink into torpor<br>陷入迟钝麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat sank the whole village into a heavy afternoon torpor.  暑热让整个村子陷入沉沉的午后麻木。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apathy</td><td>/ˈæpəθi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏兴趣，不关心</td><td>public apathy toward voting<br>公众对投票的冷漠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Widespread apathy among young voters worried the campaign organizers.  年轻选民中普遍的冷漠让竞选组织者担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 casualness</td><td>/ˈkæʒuəlnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>随意，漫不经心</td><td>surprising casualness<br>令人意外的随意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He announced the layoffs with shocking casualness.  他以令人震惊的漫不经心宣布了裁员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 complacence</del></td><td>/kəmˈpleɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自满，安于现状</td><td>dangerous complacence<br>危险的自满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early success bred a complacence that ruined the second album.  早期的成功滋生了自满，毁了第二张专辑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disinterestedness</del></td><td>/dɪsˈɪntrəstədnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不感兴趣；无私公正</td><td>an air of disinterestedness<br>一副漠不关心的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His apparent disinterestedness hid a very strong personal ambition.  他表面的漠不关心掩藏着极强的个人野心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incuriosity</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkjʊriˈɑːsəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏好奇，不感兴趣</td><td>incuriosity about the world<br>对世界缺乏好奇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her incuriosity about other cultures surprised her traveling companions.  她对其他文化缺乏好奇，令旅伴们惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insouciance</td><td>/ɪnˈsuːsiəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无忧无虑，漠不关心</td><td>an air of insouciance<br>一副满不在乎的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the criticism with complete insouciance and a shrug.  他满不在乎地耸耸肩回应批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonchalance</td><td>/ˌnɑːnʃəˈlɑːns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冷漠，漠不关心</td><td>studied nonchalance<br>刻意的淡然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the important award with surprising nonchalance.  她以出人意料的淡然接受了这个重要奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languor</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>懒惰；衰弱</td><td>summer languor<br>夏日的倦怠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy heat brought a languor that made work nearly impossible.  酷热带来一种倦怠，几乎使人无法工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lassitude</td><td>/ˈlæsɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疲乏，无精打采</td><td>a feeling of lassitude<br>一种倦怠感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange lassitude kept him in bed all afternoon.  一种奇怪的倦怠让他整个下午都躺在床上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 listlessness</td><td>/ˈlɪstləsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倦怠，无精打采</td><td>an air of listlessness<br>一副无精打采的样子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange listlessness settled over the office in August.  八月里办公室弥漫着一种奇怪的倦怠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stupor</td><td>/ˈstuːpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迟钝，麻痹，无知觉</td><td>fall into a stupor<br>陷入麻木状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a stupor and did not respond to our questions.  他陷入麻木状态，对我们的问题毫无反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concern</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关心，担心；涉及</td><td>show genuine concern<br>表现出真切的关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents expressed concern about the safety of the playground.  家长们对操场的安全表示关心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interest</td><td>/ˈɪntrəst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>兴趣，关注；利息</td><td>show interest in music<br>对音乐表现出兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She lost interest in the project after only a month.  仅一个月后她就对这个项目失去了兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regard</td><td>/rɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>关注，重视；看待</td><td>have regard for others<br>顾及他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drives fast with little regard for other people.  他开车很快，几乎不顾及他人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigor</td><td>/ˈvɪɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>youthful vigor<br>青春活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She argued her case with surprising vigor after a sleepless night.  一夜未眠后，她仍以惊人的活力申辩自己的主张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vim</td><td>/vɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，精力</td><td>full of vim and vigor<br>精力充沛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At seventy he still worked with surprising vim and good humor.  七十岁的他干活仍精力惊人，心情愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitality</td><td>/vaɪˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活力，生命力</td><td>brim with vitality<br>充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old market still brims with vitality on early summer mornings.  夏日清晨，这座老市场依然充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vivacity</td><td>/vɪˈvæsəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>活泼，快活</td><td>youthful vivacity<br>年轻人的活泼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her vivacity lit up an otherwise dull dinner.  她的活泼点亮了原本乏味的晚宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>torrential</b></td><td>/təˈrenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾泻的，如注的；猛烈的</td><td>torrential rain<br>倾盆大雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Torrential rain flooded the narrow streets within half an hour.  倾盆大雨半小时内就淹了狭窄的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trickling</td><td>/ˈtrɪklɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>滴流的，细流的</td><td>a trickling stream<br>细细的小溪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By August the river was only a trickling stream over stones.  到八月，那条河只剩下石头上的一道细流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>torrid</b></td><td>/ˈtɔːrɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的，情感深厚的</td><td>the torrid zone<br>热带地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The torrid afternoon heat kept everyone indoors until the sun finally set.  酷热的午后让所有人待在室内，直到太阳终于落下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hot</td><td>/ˈhɑːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>炎热的；辣的</td><td>a hot afternoon<br>炎热的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was far too hot to work outside after noon.  午后太热，根本无法在户外工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scalding</td><td>/ˈskɔːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滚烫的；（言辞）严厉的</td><td>scalding criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a scalding review of the opening night.  评论家为首演之夜写了一篇严厉的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scorching</td><td>/ˈskɔːrtʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的，灼热的</td><td>a scorching summer day<br>酷热的夏日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They crossed the desert under a scorching midday sun.  他们在酷热的正午阳光下穿越沙漠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sweltering</td><td>/ˈsweltərɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的</td><td>a sweltering afternoon<br>酷热的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stayed indoors through the sweltering afternoon with the fans running.  酷热的午后我们开着风扇待在屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ardent</td><td>/ˈɑːrdənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热切的</td><td>an ardent supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an ardent fan of the team for fifty years.  他五十年来一直是这支球队的狂热球迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burning</td><td>/ˈbɜːrnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迫切的；燃烧的</td><td>a burning question<br>迫切的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Housing remains the burning issue in this crowded district.  住房仍是这个拥挤城区最迫切的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fervid</td><td>/ˈfɜːrvɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>酷热的；热情的</td><td>a fervid supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fervid speech moved even the quietest people in the room.  他热情洋溢的演讲连房间里最安静的人都被感动了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaming</td><td>/ˈfleɪmɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炽热的，火焰般的</td><td>flaming summer heat<br>炽热的夏日高温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flaming afternoon sun made the empty market street unbearable.  炽热的午后阳光让空荡的集市街道令人难以忍受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glowing</td><td>/ˈɡloʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，炽热的</td><td>glowing embers<br>发光的余烬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Glowing coals gave the little room a warm orange light.  发光的炭火给小屋带来温暖的橙色光线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impassioned</td><td>/ɪmˈpæʃənd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨激昂的，充满激情的</td><td>an impassioned speech<br>慷慨激昂的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an impassioned defense of public libraries.  她慷慨激昂地为公共图书馆辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incandescent</td><td>/ˌɪnkænˈdesənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>白热的，耀眼的</td><td>an incandescent bulb<br>白炽灯泡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incandescent filament burned out after only a few weeks.  白炽灯丝仅用几周就烧断了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intense</td><td>/ɪnˈtens/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强烈的，极度的</td><td>intense heat<br>酷热</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The intense heat inside the foundry forced the workers to rest hourly.  铸造厂内的酷热迫使工人每小时休息一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passionate</td><td>/ˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，充满激情的</td><td>a passionate defense of art<br>对艺术的热情辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is passionate about protecting the city's old neighborhoods.  她对保护城市的老街区充满热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vehement</td><td>/ˈviːəmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情感）强烈的，热情的</td><td>vehement opposition<br>强烈的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a vehement protest against the new tax.  她对新税提出了强烈的抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arctic</td><td>/ˈɑːrktɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极寒的；北极的</td><td>arctic temperatures<br>极寒的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An arctic wind swept across the empty parking lot.  一阵极寒的风扫过空荡的停车场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bone-chilling</td><td>/ˈboʊn ˌtʃɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>寒冷刺骨的</td><td>a bone-chilling wind<br>刺骨的寒风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bone-chilling wind swept across the empty parking lot.  一阵刺骨的寒风扫过空荡的停车场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 freezing</td><td>/ˈfriːzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的，冰冻的</td><td>freezing temperatures<br>冰点以下的气温</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We waited on the freezing platform for half an hour.  我们在极冷的站台上等了半小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frigid</td><td>/ˈfrɪdʒɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒的；冷漠的</td><td>a frigid stare<br>冷冰冰的凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the latecomer a frigid stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地盯了迟到者一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frozen</td><td>/ˈfroʊzən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冻结的，冰冻的</td><td>a frozen lake<br>结冰的湖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children skated across the frozen pond behind the church.  孩子们在教堂后结冰的池塘上滑冰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glacial</td><td>/ˈɡleɪsiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极冷的；冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>a glacial silence<br>冰冷的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His glacial tone made everyone at the table stop talking at once.  他冰冷的语气让桌边所有人立刻停止了交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 icy</td><td>/ˈaɪsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冰冷的；冷淡的</td><td>an icy road<br>结冰的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave him an icy stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地瞪了他一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dispassionate</td><td>/dɪsˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静客观的，不带感情的</td><td>a dispassionate analysis<br>冷静客观的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge gave a dispassionate summary of both arguments.  法官冷静客观地总结了双方的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 emotionless</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊʃənləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感情的，冷漠的</td><td>an emotionless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He read the verdict in a flat, emotionless tone.  他用平板毫无感情的语调宣读了判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impassive</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>remain impassive under pressure<br>压力下仍无动于衷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face stayed impassive as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，他的脸上始终毫无表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unemotional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈmoʊʃənəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动感情的，冷静的</td><td>an unemotional account<br>不带感情的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unemotional summary of the disaster unsettled the listeners.  她不带感情的灾难总结让听众不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>tortuous</b></td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>转弯抹角的</td><td>a tortuous argument<br>转弯抹角的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His tortuous explanation left the whole committee more confused than before.  他转弯抹角的解释让整个委员会比之前更困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bending</td><td>/ˈbendɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>弯曲的，转弯的</td><td>a bending stretch of road<br>弯曲的一段路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bending river hid the village from view.  弯曲的河流把村子挡在视野之外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curling</td><td>/ˈkɜːrlɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卷曲的，弯绕的</td><td>curling smoke<br>袅袅卷曲的烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curling vines covered the whole stone wall.  卷曲的藤蔓覆盖了整面石墙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curvy</td><td>/ˈkɜːrvi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多曲线的，弯曲的</td><td>a curvy coastal road<br>弯弯曲曲的海滨公路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We drove slowly along the curvy road above the sea.  我们沿海边弯曲的公路慢慢开车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devious</td><td>/ˈdiːviəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的</td><td>devious tactics<br>狡诈的手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used devious means to obtain a copy of the secret contract.  他用狡猾的手段弄到了那份秘密合同的副本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 serpentine</td><td>/ˈsɜːrpəntiːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>弯曲的</td><td>a serpentine mountain road<br>蜿蜒的山路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We drove slowly along a serpentine mountain road above the valley.  我们沿着山谷上方蜿蜒的山路缓慢前行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sinuous</td><td>/ˈsɪnjuəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>蜿蜒的，迂回的</td><td>a sinuous river<br>蜿蜒的河流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sinuous river winds through the wide valley below the village.  一条蜿蜒的河流穿过村子下方宽阔的谷地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twisted</td><td>/ˈtwɪstɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扭曲的；反常的</td><td>a twisted plot<br>曲折离奇的情节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's twisted plot keeps readers guessing until the last page.  这部小说曲折的情节让读者猜到最后一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 windy</td><td>/ˈwɪndi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的</td><td>a windy speech<br>冗长空洞的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His windy speech said very little in more than an hour.  他冗长的演讲讲了一个多小时却几乎没什么内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 direct</td><td>/dəˈrekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直接的，径直的</td><td>a direct flight<br>直飞航班</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ask for a direct answer instead of a long explanation.  要个直接的答复，而不是长篇解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straightforward</td><td>/ˌstreɪtˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单明了的；坦率的</td><td>a straightforward procedure<br>简单直接的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions are straightforward enough for a child to follow.  说明简单明了，连孩子都能照着做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>tout</b></td><td>/taʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极力赞扬</td><td>tout a new product<br>极力吹捧新产品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Advertisements tout the new drug as a cure for nearly every ailment.  广告把这种新药吹捧成几乎万能的良药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ballyhoo</td><td>/ˈbælihuː/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>大肆宣传，喧闹的吹捧</td><td>media ballyhoo<br>媒体的大肆宣传</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film arrived with enormous ballyhoo but disappointed almost everyone.  这部电影在大肆宣传中上映，却几乎让所有人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 asperse</del></td><td>/əˈspɜrs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>asperse his character<br>中伤他的品格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet aspersed every officer who supported the reform.  这本小册子中伤了每一位支持改革的官员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 denounce</td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 berate</td><td>/bɪˈreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉指责，斥责</td><td>berate him publicly<br>公开斥责他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach began to berate his players in front of reporters.  教练开始在记者面前严厉斥责他的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rate</td><td>/ˈreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>评价，认为值得</td><td>rate him highly<br>对他评价很高</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics rate her early novels far above the later ones.  评论家对她早期的小说评价远高于后期作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revile</td><td>/riˈvaɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>辱骂，斥责</td><td>revile his opponents<br>辱骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newspapers reviled the mayor for weeks after the scandal broke.  丑闻曝光后报纸辱骂市长数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 vituperate</del></td><td>/vaɪˈtuːpəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谩骂，责骂</td><td>vituperate an opponent<br>谩骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist vituperated the mayor in every weekly article.  这位专栏作家在每周的文章里都痛骂市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>toy</b></td><td>/tɔɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>草率或不认真地对待</td><td>toy with the idea<br>不认真地考虑该想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She toyed with the idea of moving abroad but never made real plans.  她曾不认真地考虑过移居国外，却从未真正计划过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diddle with</td><td>/ˈdɪdəl wɪð/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>胡乱摆弄，瞎调整</td><td>diddle with the settings<br>乱动设置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stop diddling with the settings or you will break the camera.  别再乱动设置，不然你会把相机弄坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiddle with</td><td>/ˈfɪdəl wɪð/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>摆弄，乱动</td><td>fiddle with the controls<br>乱动控制装置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fiddled with the radio dial until the static disappeared.  他摆弄收音机旋钮，直到杂音消失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fool with</td><td>/ˈful wɪð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>瞎弄，胡乱摆弄</td><td>fool with the wiring<br>瞎动电线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Never fool with the wiring while the power is still on.  电源没断时千万不要瞎动电线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mess with</td><td>/ˈmɛs wɪð/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>乱动，胡乱干预</td><td>mess with my files<br>乱动我的文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Someone messed with my files and deleted half the folder.  有人乱动我的文件，删掉了半个文件夹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monkey with</td><td>/ˈmʌŋki wɪð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>瞎摆弄，乱动</td><td>monkey with the settings<br>乱动设置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please stop monkeying with the thermostat every time you walk past.  别每次走过都去乱动恒温器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tinker with</td><td>/ˈtɪŋkər wɪð/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>小修小改，摆弄</td><td>tinker with the engine<br>摆弄发动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the weekend tinkering with an old radio in the garage.  他整个周末在车库里摆弄一台旧收音机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twiddle with</td><td>/ˈtwɪdəl wɪð/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>摆弄，无目的地拨弄</td><td>twiddle with the knobs<br>拨弄旋钮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She twiddled with the knobs until the picture finally cleared.  她拨弄旋钮，画面终于清晰了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>tractable</b></td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manageable</td><td>/ˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易管理的，好对付的</td><td>a manageable workload<br>可应付的工作量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Split the task into smaller, manageable pieces first.  先把任务拆成较小、好处理的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tame</td><td>/teɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>被驯化的；控制，抑制</td><td>tame the wild horse<br>驯服野马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the trainer months to tame the wild horse completely.  训马师花了几个月才彻底驯服那匹野马。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disobedient</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，不听话的</td><td>a disobedient child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disobedient dog ran off the moment the gate opened.  门一开，那只不听话的狗就跑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 incompliant</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顺从的，不依从的</td><td>an incompliant witness<br>不肯配合的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incompliant staff refused to follow the new dress rule.  不顺从的员工拒绝遵守新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 restive</td><td>/ˈrestɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的；不驯服的</td><td>a restive crowd<br>焦躁不安的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience became restive when the concert started forty minutes late.  音乐会迟了四十分钟才开始，观众变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unamenable</del></td><td>/ˌʌnəˈminəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顺从的，不易接受的</td><td>unamenable to persuasion<br>劝不动的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old machine proved unamenable to any modern repair method.  事实证明这台旧机器不适用于任何现代修理方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ungovernable</td><td>/ʌnˈɡʌvərnəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以控制的，无法管束的</td><td>an ungovernable temper<br>无法控制的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd became ungovernable once the gates were opened.  大门一开，人群就变得无法控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 untoward</td><td>/ʌnˈtɔːrd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的；难以驯服的，难以驾驭的</td><td>an untoward incident<br>意外的不幸事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing untoward happened during the long flight across the ocean.  在这趟跨洋长途飞行中没有发生任何不幸的意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wayward</td><td>/ˈweɪwərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，不服管束的</td><td>a wayward son<br>不服管束的儿子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their wayward son ignored every rule his patient parents set.  他们不服管束的儿子无视父母耐心定下的每条规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>tranquility</b></td><td>/træŋˈkwɪləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，淡定</td><td>an air of tranquility<br>宁静的气息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enjoyed the tranquility of the snowy fields at dusk.  他享受黄昏时白雪覆盖的田野的静谧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silence</td><td>/ˈsaɪləns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>寂静，沉默；使沉默</td><td>break the silence<br>打破沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long silence followed his surprising announcement to the class.  他向全班宣布这惊人消息后是长久的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 serenity</td><td>/səˈrenəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，安详</td><td>a sense of serenity<br>一种宁静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden offers a rare serenity in the middle of the city.  这座花园在城市中心提供了难得的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bustle</td><td>/ˈbʌsl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>忙乱，喧闹；（快速地）行走，奔忙</td><td>the bustle of the street<br>街上的喧闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love the bustle of the market on Saturday mornings.  我喜欢周六早晨市场上的喧闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commotion</td><td>/kəˈmoʊʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>骚乱</td><td>cause a commotion<br>引起一阵骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The escaped goat caused quite a commotion in the market.  逃跑的山羊在市场上引起一阵不小的骚动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hubbub</td><td>/ˈhʌbəb/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹，嘈杂；骚动</td><td>the hubbub of the market<br>市场的喧闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We could barely talk above the hubbub of the crowd.  在人群的喧闹中我们几乎无法交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pandemonium</td><td>/ˌpændɪˈmoʊniəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，一片混乱</td><td>pandemonium broke out<br>顿时一片混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pandemonium broke out when the final goal was scored.  最后一个进球时全场一片沸腾混乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tumult</td><td>/ˈtuːmʌlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚动，暴动</td><td>the tumult of the crowd<br>人群的骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tumult in the square grew louder as more protesters arrived.  随着更多抗议者到来，广场上的骚动越来越大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 turmoil</td><td>/ˈtɜːrmɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混乱，动荡，骚动</td><td>in political turmoil<br>处于政治动荡中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country remained in political turmoil for nearly a decade.  这个国家在近十年里一直处于政治动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unquietness</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkwaɪətnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不安，骚动</td><td>a growing unquietness<br>日渐加深的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange unquietness spread through the village before the storm.  暴风雨来临前，一种奇怪的不安在村里蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unrest</td><td>/ʌnˈrest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不安，动荡，骚乱</td><td>social unrest<br>社会动荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising food prices led to months of social unrest.  食品价格上涨导致了数月的社会动荡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>transcend</b></td><td>/trænˈsend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>超越，超过极限</td><td>transcend cultural limits<br>超越文化界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Great music can transcend the barriers of language and national culture.  伟大的音乐能超越语言和民族文化的障碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outreach</td><td>/ˈaʊtriːtʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>超出，超过</td><td>outreach all rivals<br>超越所有对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ambition outreached anything her small town could offer her.  她的抱负超出了小镇能给她的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outrun</td><td>/aʊˈtrʌn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>跑得比…快，超过</td><td>outrun the competition<br>跑赢竞争者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Demand for the vaccine quickly outran the factory's output.  疫苗的需求很快超过了工厂的产量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overpass</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌpæs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>忽略，越过</td><td>overpass minor faults<br>忽略小的过失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor overpassed several errors in the final draft.  编辑忽略了终稿中的几处错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overreach</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈriːtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>做过头，超出限度</td><td>overreach oneself<br>做事过头而失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company overreached and lost both markets within a year.  这家公司做得过头，一年内丢了两个市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overrun</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈrʌn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>超出，泛滥</td><td>overrun the budget<br>超出预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project overran its budget by nearly two million dollars.  该项目超出预算近两百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overshoot</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈʃuːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>超过目标，冲过</td><td>overshoot the target<br>超过目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plane overshot the runway and stopped on the grass.  飞机冲过跑道，停在草地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overstep</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈstep/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>越过，超出（界限）</td><td>overstep the boundary<br>越过界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer clearly overstepped his authority during the search.  这名警官在搜查中明显越权了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surpass</td><td>/sərˈpæs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>超过；胜过；优于</td><td>surpass all expectations<br>超出一切预期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist's performance surpassed all expectations of the judges.  这位年轻小提琴手的表现超出评委的一切预期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>transfigure</b></td><td>/trænsˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使改变外观</td><td>transfigure her face<br>使她面容改观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rising sunlight transfigured the plain valley into a field of gold.  初升的阳光把平凡的山谷变成一片金色的原野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 alchemize</del></td><td>/ˈælkəmaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使转化，使发生变化</td><td>alchemize grief into art<br>把悲伤化为艺术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She alchemized her hardest years into two fine novels.  她把最艰难的岁月化成了两部好小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 metamorphose</td><td>/ˌmetəˈmɔːrfoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使变形，彻底改变</td><td>metamorphose into a butterfly<br>蜕变成蝴蝶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shy student metamorphosed into a confident public speaker.  那个害羞的学生蜕变成了自信的演讲者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transform</td><td>/trænsˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底改变，转变</td><td>transform the neighborhood<br>彻底改变街区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subway line transformed the quiet neighborhood within five years.  新地铁线五年内彻底改变了这个安静的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transmute</td><td>/trænzˈmjuːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变形，使转化</td><td>transmute lead into gold<br>把铅变成黄金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Great heat transmutes the soft metal into something far harder.  高温把这种软金属变成硬得多的东西。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transpose</td><td>/trænˈspoʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>调换位置，转换</td><td>transpose two letters<br>调换两个字母的位置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The typist transposed two digits in the phone number.  打字员把电话号码里的两个数字调换了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 transubstantiate</del></td><td>/ˌtrænsəbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使实质改变，使变体</td><td>transubstantiate bread and wine<br>使饼与酒发生变体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ritual is said to transubstantiate the bread completely.  据说这一仪式使饼彻底改变了实质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 make over</td><td>/meɪk ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>改造，彻底翻新</td><td>make over the kitchen<br>改造厨房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They made over the attic into a bright little study.  他们把阁楼改造成了明亮的小书房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>transgress</b></td><td>/trænzˈɡres/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违背；犯错</td><td>transgress the rules<br>违反规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any player who transgresses the rules will be removed from the tournament.  任何违反规则的选手都将被逐出这场比赛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contravene</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈviːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违反，反对</td><td>contravene the rules<br>违反规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building a factory here would contravene several local environmental regulations.  在此建厂会违反数条当地环保法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fracture</td><td>/ˈfræktʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>破裂</td><td>a hairline fracture<br>一道细微裂缝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan revealed a hairline fracture in his left wrist.  扫描显示他左手腕有一道细微裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infringe</td><td>/ɪnˈfrɪndʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违反；侵犯（权利）</td><td>infringe on his rights<br>侵犯他的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rule infringes on the privacy of every employee.  新规定侵犯了每位员工的隐私。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offend</td><td>/əˈfend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>违犯，触犯</td><td>offend against the law<br>触犯法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement offended against every rule of good taste.  这则广告违背了一切良好品味的规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 traduce</del></td><td>/trəˈduːs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诽谤，中伤</td><td>traduce an opponent<br>中伤对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rival papers traduced the senator throughout the long campaign.  对立的报纸在整场竞选中中伤这位参议员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 follow</td><td>/ˈfɑːloʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵循，跟随</td><td>follow the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 If you follow the instructions exactly, nothing can go wrong.  只要严格按说明操作，就不会出错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mind</td><td>/ˈmaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>留意，注意；介意</td><td>mind the step<br>留意台阶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please mind the gap between the train and the platform.  请注意列车与站台之间的空隙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obey</td><td>/oʊˈbeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>obey the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drivers must obey the speed limit in school zones.  司机在学校区域必须遵守限速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 observe</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守；观察</td><td>observe the law<br>遵守法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All visitors are expected to observe the museum's rules.  所有参观者都应遵守博物馆的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comply with</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪ wɪð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守，服从</td><td>comply with regulations<br>遵守规章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every factory must comply with the new emission standards.  每家工厂都必须遵守新的排放标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conform to</td><td>/kənˈfɔrm tuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守，符合</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every part must conform to the national safety standard.  每个零件都必须符合国家安全标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>transient</b></td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transitory</td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleeting</td><td>/ˈfliːtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；转瞬即逝的</td><td>a fleeting moment<br>短暂的瞬间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For one fleeting moment the clouds parted above the valley.  有一个短暂的瞬间，云在山谷上方散开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temporary</td><td>/ˈtempəreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时的，临时的</td><td>a temporary solution<br>临时的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company hired temporary staff to handle the busy holiday season.  公司雇了临时员工来应对繁忙的假日季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dateless</td><td>/ˈdeɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老得无从考证的</td><td>dateless folk songs<br>年代不明的民歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These dateless legends were told long before anyone wrote them down.  这些年代不明的传说早在有人记录之前就流传着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deathless</td><td>/ˈdɛθləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不死的，不朽的</td><td>deathless fame<br>不朽的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet won deathless fame with a single slender volume.  这位诗人凭一本薄薄的诗集赢得了不朽名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 endless</td><td>/ˈendləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，无休止的</td><td>endless complaints<br>无休止的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting felt endless, though it lasted only ninety minutes.  会议感觉没完没了，其实只开了九十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enduring</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长存的</td><td>an enduring friendship<br>长久的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their enduring friendship survived decades of separation.  他们持久的友谊经历了数十年的分离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lasting</td><td>/ˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，永久的</td><td>a lasting impression<br>持久的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her kindness left a lasting impression on everyone in the ward.  她的善意给病房里每个人留下持久印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 long-lived</td><td>/ˌlɔŋ ˈlaɪvd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>长寿的，持久的</td><td>a long-lived tradition<br>延续久远的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tortoises are famously long-lived compared with most other animals.  与大多数动物相比，乌龟以长寿闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 timeless</td><td>/ˈtaɪmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不受时间影响的</td><td>timeless design<br>经典不过时的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dress has a timeless elegance that never dates.  这条裙子有一种永不过时的优雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unending</td><td>/ʌnˈendɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，不停的</td><td>an unending stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unending stream of visitors filled the museum all summer.  整个夏天络绎不绝的游客挤满了博物馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>transitory</b></td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleeting</td><td>/ˈfliːtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；转瞬即逝的</td><td>a fleeting moment<br>短暂的瞬间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For one fleeting moment the clouds parted above the valley.  有一个短暂的瞬间，云在山谷上方散开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temporary</td><td>/ˈtempəreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暂时的，临时的</td><td>a temporary solution<br>临时的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company hired temporary staff to handle the busy holiday season.  公司雇了临时员工来应对繁忙的假日季。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ceaseless</td><td>/ˈsiːslɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不停的，持续的</td><td>ceaseless noise<br>不停的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceaseless traffic outside made it impossible for us to sleep.  外面不停的车流让我们无法入睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dateless</td><td>/ˈdeɪtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老得无从考证的</td><td>dateless folk songs<br>年代不明的民歌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These dateless legends were told long before anyone wrote them down.  这些年代不明的传说早在有人记录之前就流传着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deathless</td><td>/ˈdɛθləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不死的，不朽的</td><td>deathless fame<br>不朽的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet won deathless fame with a single slender volume.  这位诗人凭一本薄薄的诗集赢得了不朽名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 endless</td><td>/ˈendləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，无休止的</td><td>endless complaints<br>无休止的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting felt endless, though it lasted only ninety minutes.  会议感觉没完没了，其实只开了九十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enduring</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长存的</td><td>an enduring friendship<br>长久的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their enduring friendship survived decades of separation.  他们持久的友谊经历了数十年的分离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lasting</td><td>/ˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，永久的</td><td>a lasting impression<br>持久的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her kindness left a lasting impression on everyone in the ward.  她的善意给病房里每个人留下持久印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 long-lived</td><td>/ˌlɔŋ ˈlaɪvd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>长寿的，持久的</td><td>a long-lived tradition<br>延续久远的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tortoises are famously long-lived compared with most other animals.  与大多数动物相比，乌龟以长寿闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 timeless</td><td>/ˈtaɪmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不受时间影响的</td><td>timeless design<br>经典不过时的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dress has a timeless elegance that never dates.  这条裙子有一种永不过时的优雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unending</td><td>/ʌnˈendɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无尽的，不停的</td><td>an unending stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unending stream of visitors filled the museum all summer.  整个夏天络绎不绝的游客挤满了博物馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>translucent</b></td><td>/trænsˈluːsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>半透明的</td><td>translucent glass panels<br>半透明的玻璃板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Morning light glowed softly through the translucent paper screens.  晨光柔和地透过半透明的纸屏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transparent</td><td>/trænsˈpærənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有歧义的，清晰易懂的</td><td>a transparent explanation<br>清晰易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a transparent explanation that even beginners understood immediately.  他给出清晰易懂的解释，连初学者都立刻明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limpid</td><td>/ˈlɪmpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>透明清澈的</td><td>a limpid mountain stream<br>清澈的山间溪流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The limpid water let us count stones on the bottom.  清澈的水让我们能数清水底的石头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pellucid</td><td>/pəˈluːsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰明确的，易懂的</td><td>pellucid prose<br>明白易懂的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pellucid explanation made the theory seem obvious.  他清晰的讲解让这个理论显得浅显易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucent</td><td>/ˈluːsɪnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明亮的，透明的</td><td>lucent morning light<br>明亮的晨光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lake lay lucent under a cloudless autumn sky.  湖水在无云的秋空下清亮透明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucid</td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 transpicuous</del></td><td>/trænˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>透明的；明白易懂的</td><td>transpicuous prose<br>明白易懂的文字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His transpicuous explanation made the hard theorem suddenly obvious.  他明白易懂的讲解让难懂的定理忽然清晰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opaque</td><td>/oʊˈpeɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不透明的；晦涩难懂的</td><td>opaque legal language<br>晦涩的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regulations remain opaque even to experienced tax lawyers.  这些法规连资深税务律师都觉得晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>transparent</b></td><td>/trænsˈpærənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有歧义的，清晰易懂的</td><td>a transparent explanation<br>清晰易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a transparent explanation that even beginners understood immediately.  他给出清晰易懂的解释，连初学者都立刻明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obvious</td><td>/ˈɑːbviəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显而易见的</td><td>an obvious mistake<br>明显的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The answer seemed obvious once she explained the method.  她解释了方法后，答案似乎显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apparent</td><td>/əˈperənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的；表面上的</td><td>for no apparent reason<br>没有明显原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It soon became apparent that the machine had never worked.  很快就明显看出这台机器从未运转过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 understandable</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈstændəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的；情有可原的</td><td>perfectly understandable<br>完全可以理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was understandable given how badly he was treated.  考虑到他受到的糟糕对待，他的愤怒可以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apprehensible</del></td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhensəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>readily apprehensible ideas<br>易于理解的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument is barely apprehensible to readers without training.  他的论证对没有受过训练的读者几乎难以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comprehensible</td><td>/ˌkɑːmpriˈhensəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，易懂的</td><td>clear and comprehensible<br>清晰易懂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers make difficult ideas comprehensible to complete beginners.  好老师能让初学者也理解艰深的概念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fathomable</del></td><td>/ˈfæðəməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，可探明的</td><td>hardly fathomable motives<br>难以捉摸的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her reasons for leaving were not easily fathomable to us.  她离开的原因我们不容易理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ambiguous</td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clouded</td><td>/ˈklaʊdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浑浊的，模糊不清的</td><td>clouded water<br>浑浊的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain left the pond clouded for several days.  雨水使池塘浑浊了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cryptic</td><td>/ˈkrɪptɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秘密的；难以理解的</td><td>a cryptic remark<br>费解的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His cryptic note gave us no hint about where he had gone.  他那张费解的便条没有透露他去了哪里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enigmatic</td><td>/ˌenɪɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>an enigmatic smile<br>神秘的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enigmatic answer left the reporters guessing for several days.  她神秘的回答让记者们猜了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indistinct</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈstɪŋkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊不清的，含糊的</td><td>an indistinct outline<br>模糊的轮廓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photograph shows an indistinct figure by the door.  照片上门边有个模糊的人影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mysterious</td><td>/mɪˈstɪriəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>a mysterious stranger<br>神秘的陌生人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A mysterious light appeared over the lake around midnight.  午夜时分湖面上出现了一道神秘的光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obfuscated</td><td>/ˈɑbfəskeɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混淆不清的，弄糊涂的</td><td>obfuscated legal language<br>晦涩的法律语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report was so obfuscated that nobody could find the real numbers.  这份报告写得如此含混，没人能找到真实数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unclarified</del></td><td>/ʌnˈklerəfaɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未澄清的，未说明的</td><td>unclarified details<br>未说明的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several unclarified points in the contract worried the lawyers.  合同中几处未澄清的地方让律师担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unclear</td><td>/ʌnˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不清楚的，不明确的</td><td>remain unclear<br>仍不明确</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is still unclear who made the final decision.  谁做的最终决定仍不清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unclouded</td><td>/ʌnˈklaʊdɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无云的，晴朗的</td><td>an unclouded sky<br>晴朗的天空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stars were vivid in the unclouded desert night.  在晴朗的沙漠夜空中，星星格外明亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>travail</b></td><td>/ˈtræveɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>辛苦劳动</td><td>years of travail<br>多年辛劳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months of travail, the small team finally finished the bridge.  经过数月的辛苦劳作，这个小团队终于建成了这座桥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drudgery</td><td>/ˈdrʌdʒəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>苦工；单调、卑贱或无趣的工作</td><td>the drudgery of housework<br>家务的苦累</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Machines have removed much of the drudgery of housework in modern homes.  机器已消除了现代家庭中大部分的家务苦累。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fatigue</td><td>/fəˈtiːɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>疲惫</td><td>suffer from constant fatigue<br>长期感到疲惫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters battling the blaze all day suffered from severe fatigue.  整天与大火搏斗的消防员疲劳不堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grind</td><td>/ˈɡraɪnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>磨碎，磨光；苦干</td><td>grind the lens smooth<br>把镜片磨光滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers ground the marble surface until it shone.  工人把大理石表面磨到发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>traverse</b></td><td>/trəˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，否认；横穿</td><td>traverse the allegation<br>反驳该指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defense traversed every allegation made by the prosecution.  辩方否认了检方提出的每一项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 course</td><td>/ˈkɔːrs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>奔流，快速行进</td><td>course through his veins<br>在他血管中奔流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Adrenaline coursed through his veins as the race finally began.  比赛终于开始时肾上腺素在他血管中奔涌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cover</td><td>/ˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，遮盖；覆盖物</td><td>cover up the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tried to cover up the truth with a vague statement.  他们试图用含糊的声明掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cross</td><td>/ˈkrɔːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>反驳，阻挠；穿越</td><td>cross his plans<br>阻挠他的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody dared cross the old chairman in public.  没人敢在公开场合反驳这位老董事长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut across</td><td>/kʌt əˈkrɔːs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>穿过；超越（界限）</td><td>cut across party lines<br>跨越党派界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Support for the reform cut across every social class in the country.  对这项改革的支持跨越了该国的各个社会阶层。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 navigate</td><td>/ˈnævəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>航行；穿行，导航</td><td>navigate the winding river<br>沿蜿蜒的河流行进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She navigated the crowded market with surprising ease.  她轻松自如地穿行于拥挤的市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pass over</td><td>/pæs ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不予考虑，忽略</td><td>pass over a candidate<br>忽略某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee passed over three senior staff and promoted a newcomer.  委员会跳过三名资深员工，提拔了一位新人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perambulate</del></td><td>/pəˈræmbjuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>徒步穿越，走过</td><td>perambulate the old town<br>徒步走遍老城</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every evening the old couple perambulate the narrow streets of the town.  每天傍晚，那对老夫妻都在镇上的窄街间徒步走一圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 peregrinate</del></td><td>/ˈpɛrəɡrəneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>徒步旅行，漫游</td><td>peregrinate through Europe<br>徒步游历欧洲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each summer he would peregrinate through the villages of northern Spain.  每年夏天他都会漫游西班牙北部的村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proceed along</td><td>/prəˈsiːd əˈlɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>沿着…前行；照…推进</td><td>proceed along the corridor<br>沿走廊前行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The negotiations proceeded along the lines both sides had agreed on.  谈判按双方商定的思路推进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transit</td><td>/ˈtrænzɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>通行，通过；运输</td><td>in transit through the city<br>过境穿城而行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The goods were damaged while in transit through the mountains.  货物在穿越山区运输途中受损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travel</td><td>/ˈtrævəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>旅行，行走</td><td>travel on foot<br>步行旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They traveled on foot across the valley for two long days.  他们徒步穿越山谷走了漫长的两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>travesty</b></td><td>/ˈtrævəsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拙劣的、嘲弄性模仿</td><td>a travesty of justice<br>对正义的拙劣讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the hasty trial a travesty of justice.  批评者称这场匆忙的审判是对正义的拙劣讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caricature</td><td>/ˈkærɪkətʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>讽刺画，夸张的滑稽模仿</td><td>draw a caricature<br>画一幅讽刺画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cartoonist drew a cruel caricature of the new mayor.  漫画家画了一幅刻薄的新市长讽刺画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burlesque</td><td>/bɜːrˈlesk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>滑稽夸张的模仿讽刺作品；恶搞</td><td>a burlesque of politics<br>对政治的滑稽恶搞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play is a clever burlesque of modern political debate.  这部戏是对当代政治辩论的巧妙滑稽模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parody</td><td>/ˈpærədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（以嘲笑原作作者的）模仿作品，恶搞</td><td>a parody of the film<br>对该片的恶搞模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students wrote a hilarious parody of the morning speech at assembly.  学生们写了一篇滑稽的作品，恶搞集会上的早间讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spoof</td><td>/spuːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松幽默的模仿，小恶搞</td><td>a spoof of the news<br>对新闻的幽默模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The show aired a clever spoof of the evening news broadcast.  节目播出了对晚间新闻的一段巧妙恶搞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mock</td><td>/ˈmɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>假的，模拟的；嘲笑</td><td>a mock trial<br>模拟审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students held a mock election to learn how voting works.  学生举行模拟选举以了解投票如何运作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 paragon</td><td>/ˈpærəɡɑːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>优秀模范；把…比作；显示相似</td><td>a paragon of patience<br>耐心的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old librarian was a paragon of patience with every noisy student.  那位老图书馆员对每个吵闹的学生都是耐心的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>treacherous</b></td><td>/ˈtretʃərəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>背信弃义的；（路况等）危险的</td><td>a treacherous ally<br>背信弃义的盟友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A treacherous adviser sold the king's battle plans to the enemy.  一个背信弃义的谋士把国王的作战计划卖给了敌人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faithless</td><td>/ˈfeɪθləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，背信的</td><td>a faithless ally<br>不忠的盟友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king punished his faithless advisers without any mercy.  国王毫不留情地惩罚了背信的谋臣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disloyal</td><td>/dɪˈslɔɪəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，背信的</td><td>disloyal to a friend<br>对朋友不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sharing those emails was disloyal to everyone on the team.  分享那些邮件是对团队所有人的背叛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfidious</td><td>/pərˈfɪdiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>背信弃义的，奸诈的</td><td>a perfidious friend<br>背信弃义的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a perfidious partner in every treaty.  历史记住他是每份条约中背信弃义的伙伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 recreant</del></td><td>/ˈrɛkriənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>背叛者，怯懦的叛徒</td><td>branded a recreant<br>被打上叛徒烙印</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ballad calls him a recreant who fled the battle.  这首民谣称他是逃离战场的怯懦叛徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 traitorous</td><td>/ˈtreɪtərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>叛国的，背叛的</td><td>traitorous conduct<br>背叛的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was executed for traitorous dealings with the enemy.  这名军官因与敌人的叛国交易被处死。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfaithful</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪθfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，不忠实的</td><td>an unfaithful servant<br>不忠的仆人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He proved unfaithful to the promises he had already signed.  他背弃了自己已签下的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unloyal</del></td><td>/ʌnˈlɔɪəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，背信的</td><td>an unloyal friend<br>不忠的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was branded unloyal for leaking the plan to a competitor.  他因把计划泄露给竞争对手而被视为不忠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 betraying</td><td>/bɪˈtreɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>背叛的，出卖的</td><td>a betraying whisper<br>出卖人的低语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One betraying glance told the guards exactly where he was hiding.  一个出卖他的眼神让守卫知道了他的藏身处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dedicated</td><td>/ˈdedəkeɪtəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>献身的，尽心的；专用的</td><td>a dedicated teacher<br>尽心尽力的教师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is dedicated to protecting the birds of this marsh.  她致力于保护这片沼泽的鸟类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 devoted</td><td>/dɪˈvoʊtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>投入的；忠诚的</td><td>a devoted follower<br>忠诚的追随者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a devoted teacher who remembers every student by name.  她是位投入的教师，能叫出每个学生的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 staunch</td><td>/stɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忠诚的，坚定的</td><td>a staunch supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A staunch supporter of reform, she never wavered under public pressure.  作为改革的坚定支持者，她从未在公众压力下动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 down-the-line</td><td>/ˌdaʊn ðə ˈlaɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彻底忠诚的，全力支持的</td><td>down-the-line support<br>毫无保留的支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the new policy her down-the-line support at the meeting.  会上她对新政策给予毫无保留的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 faithful</td><td>/ˈfeɪθfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忠实的；如实的</td><td>a faithful translation<br>忠实的译文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is a faithful adaptation of the original novel.  这部电影是对原著小说的忠实改编。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fast</td><td>/fæst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的，快的</td><td>a fast runner<br>跑得快的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a fast worker who finishes every job ahead of schedule.  他做事很快，每项工作都提前完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loyal</td><td>/ˈlɔɪəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忠诚的，忠实的</td><td>a loyal customer<br>忠实的客户</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained loyal to the team even during its worst season.  即使在最糟的赛季，他仍然忠于这支球队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steadfast</td><td>/ˈstedfæst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，忠诚的</td><td>steadfast in his belief<br>信念坚定不移</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained steadfast in her belief despite years of public ridicule.  尽管多年遭受公众嘲笑，她依然坚定地坚持自己的信念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steady</td><td>/ˈstedi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稳定的，持续不变的</td><td>a steady stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales have shown steady growth for eight straight quarters.  销售额已连续八个季度稳定增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 true</td><td>/ˈtruː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a true story<br>一个真实的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a true story from wartime.  这部电影取材于战时的一个真实故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>trenchant</b></td><td>/ˈtrentʃənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言辞）一针见血的</td><td>a trenchant critique<br>一针见血的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His trenchant remarks left the panel with nothing to add.  他一针见血的评论让评委无话可补。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incisive</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的，深刻的</td><td>an incisive analysis<br>深刻的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her incisive questions exposed the weakness of the proposal.  她敏锐的提问暴露了该提案的弱点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vague</td><td>/veɪɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达不清的</td><td>a vague answer<br>含糊的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His vague answer told the reporters almost nothing about the plan.  他含糊的回答几乎没有向记者透露任何计划内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>trepidation</b></td><td>/ˌtrepɪˈdeɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恐惧，战栗</td><td>with some trepidation<br>怀着几分恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She opened the letter with obvious trepidation, fearing the worst news.  她带着明显的恐惧打开信，担心是最坏的消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fear</td><td>/ˈfɪr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>恐惧，害怕</td><td>fear of failure<br>对失败的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fear of speaking in public gradually faded with practice.  他对公开演讲的恐惧随练习逐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fright</td><td>/ˈfraɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>惊恐，恐惧</td><td>give someone a fright<br>让某人吓一跳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden bang gave the children a terrible fright.  突然的巨响让孩子们大惊失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 horror</td><td>/ˈhɔːrər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恐怖，惊骇</td><td>watch in horror<br>惊骇地看着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers watched in horror as the bridge collapsed.  村民惊骇地看着桥塌了下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprehension</td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhenʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>忧虑，担心；逮捕</td><td>a sense of apprehension<br>忧虑感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She waited for the results with growing apprehension.  她越来越忧虑地等待结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
