<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncivil</td><td>/ʌnˈsɪvəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不礼貌的，粗鲁的</td><td>an uncivil remark<br>粗鲁的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clerk's uncivil answer made the customer complain.  职员粗鲁的回答让客户投诉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrefined</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，未提炼的</td><td>unrefined manners<br>粗俗的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unrefined manners embarrassed the whole table.  他粗俗的举止让整桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cultivated</td><td>/ˈkʌltɪveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有修养的</td><td>a cultivated taste in art<br>高雅的艺术品位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cultivated manners impressed everyone at the dinner.  她优雅的举止让晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cultured</td><td>/ˈkʌltʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有修养的</td><td>a cultured gentleman<br>有教养的绅士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was a cultured man who read poetry in four languages.  他是个有教养的人，能读四种语言的诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seemly</td><td>/ˈsiːmli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，合宜的</td><td>seemly conduct in public<br>公共场合得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher expects seemly conduct from her students in public places.  老师期望学生在公共场所举止得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unctuous</b></td><td>/ˈʌŋktʃuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>油嘴滑舌的，谄媚的</td><td>an unctuous compliment<br>油腻的奉承</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unctuous salesman praised every choice we made in his store.  那个油嘴滑舌的销售员对我们在他店里的每个选择都大加赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adulatory</del></td><td>/ˈædʒələˌtɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，过分吹捧的</td><td>an adulatory review<br>吹捧的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine ran an adulatory profile of the young director.  杂志刊登了一篇吹捧那位年轻导演的报道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gushing</td><td>/ˈɡʌʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>喷涌的；过分热情的</td><td>a gushing spring<br>喷涌的泉水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gushing spring supplies the whole village with water.  一股喷涌的泉水供给全村用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hagiographic</td><td>/ˌhæɡiəˈɡræfɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>捧为圣人一般的，过分谄媚的</td><td>a hagiographic biography<br>圣徒传式的传记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biography is so hagiographic that it mentions no flaws at all.  这本传记谄媚得如同圣徒传，完全不提任何缺点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oily</td><td>/ˈɔɪli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>油滑的，谄媚的；油质的</td><td>an oily salesman<br>油嘴滑舌的推销员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His oily compliments made everyone in the room uncomfortable.  他油滑的奉承让屋里每个人都不舒服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oleaginous</td><td>/ˌoʊliˈædʒənəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>油滑谄媚的</td><td>an oleaginous salesman<br>油嘴滑舌的销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The oleaginous host praised every guest far too warmly.  那位油滑的主持人对每位嘉宾都夸得过火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soapy</td><td>/ˈsoʊpi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的；含皂的</td><td>a soapy compliment<br>谄媚的赞美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His soapy manner with the boss embarrassed his colleagues.  他对老板谄媚的样子让同事们难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>upbeat</b></td><td>/ˈʌpbiːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观向上的，令人振奋的</td><td>an upbeat mood<br>乐观的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite the heavy loss, the coach stayed upbeat about the coming season.  尽管惨败，教练对下个赛季依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blithe</td><td>/blaɪð/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快高兴的；无忧无虑的；漫不经心的</td><td>a blithe disregard<br>漫不经心的无视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His blithe disregard for the rules annoyed the older staff.  他对规则漫不经心的无视让资深员工不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buoyant</td><td>/ˈbuːjənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心情好的</td><td>in a buoyant mood<br>心情愉快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a buoyant mood after hearing the good news.  听到好消息后，他心情愉快地到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chipper</td><td>/ˈtʃɪpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼快活的，精神好的</td><td>feel chipper this morning<br>今早心情不错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sounded surprisingly chipper for someone who slept three hours.  对只睡了三小时的人来说，她听起来出奇地精神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 eupeptic</del></td><td>/juˈpɛptɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观愉快的；消化良好的</td><td>a eupeptic mood<br>愉快乐观的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His eupeptic outlook made even bad news seem manageable.  他乐观的心态让坏消息也显得可以应付。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gay</td><td>/ˈɡeɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，欢快的</td><td>gay laughter<br>欢快的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gay music and bright lanterns filled the village square that evening.  那晚欢快的音乐和明亮的灯笼充满了村中广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lightsome</del></td><td>/ˈlaɪtsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻快的，快活的</td><td>a lightsome step<br>轻快的步子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She crossed the room with a lightsome, dancing step.  她以轻快如舞的步子穿过房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 winsome</td><td>/ˈwɪnsəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的，漂亮的</td><td>a winsome smile<br>迷人的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her winsome smile made the shy children relax almost at once.  她迷人的微笑让害羞的孩子们几乎立刻放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 auspicious</td><td>/ɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好兆头的</td><td>an auspicious beginning<br>吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain on the wedding day was considered an auspicious beginning.  婚礼当天下雨被认为是个好兆头的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encouraging</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人鼓舞的</td><td>encouraging early results<br>令人鼓舞的初步结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first trial produced encouraging results for the new treatment.  首轮试验为这种新疗法带来了令人鼓舞的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fair</td><td>/ˈfer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的；相当的；晴朗的</td><td>a fair trial<br>公正的审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone deserves a fair hearing before any decision is made.  在做出任何决定之前，人人都应得到公正的申辩机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heartening</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtənɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人振奋的</td><td>a heartening response<br>令人振奋的反响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was heartening to see strangers helping clear the flooded street.  看到陌生人一起清理被淹的街道令人振奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 optimistic</td><td>/ˌɑːptəˈmɪstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的</td><td>remain cautiously optimistic<br>保持谨慎乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite the losses, the founders remained optimistic about next year.  尽管亏损，创始人们对明年依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promising</td><td>/ˈprɑːməsɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有前途的，有希望的</td><td>a promising young writer<br>有前途的青年作家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is the most promising violinist the school has ever trained.  她是这所学校培养过的最有前途的小提琴手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propitious</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吉祥的</td><td>a propitious moment<br>吉利的时机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They waited for a propitious moment to announce the merger.  他们等待一个吉利的时机来宣布合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dour</td><td>/ˈdaʊər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>a dour expression<br>严厉的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard's dour expression discouraged anyone from asking him for directions.  警卫严厉的表情让人不敢向他问路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gloomy</td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glum</td><td>/ˈɡlʌm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的，忧闷的</td><td>a glum expression<br>阴郁的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat in the corner with a glum expression all evening.  他整晚坐在角落里，一脸阴郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 morose</td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 saturnine</td><td>/ˈsætərnaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的，阴沉的</td><td>a saturnine temperament<br>阴沉的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His saturnine expression discouraged anyone from starting a cheerful conversation.  他阴沉的表情让人不敢开启轻松的话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sulky</td><td>/ˈsʌlki/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>赌气的，生气不说话的</td><td>a sulky teenager<br>赌气的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sulky child refused to speak for the whole long afternoon.  那个赌气的孩子整个下午都不肯说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sullen</td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bleak</td><td>/ˈbliːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）阴冷的；荒凉的</td><td>a bleak winter day<br>阴冷的冬日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The moor looked bleak under grey November skies.  灰暗的十一月天空下，荒原显得阴冷荒凉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dark</td><td>/ˈdɑːrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的；黑暗的</td><td>a dark mood<br>阴郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a dark mood after reading the letter.  读了那封信后他陷入阴郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depressing</td><td>/dɪˈpresɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>a depressing forecast<br>令人沮丧的预测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy weather and empty streets felt deeply depressing.  阴雨天气和空荡的街道让人非常压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 desperate</td><td>/ˈdesprɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>绝望的，走投无路的</td><td>a desperate situation<br>绝望的处境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the flood the families were desperate and almost without hope.  洪水过后，这些家庭走投无路，几乎毫无希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dismal</td><td>/ˈdɪzməl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴沉的，凄凉的</td><td>a dismal failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dismal gray sky matched everyone's mood.  阴沉灰暗的天空与众人的心情相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>undergird</b></td><td>/ˌʌndərˈɡɜːrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支撑，为…打下基础</td><td>undergird the theory<br>为理论打牢基础</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful field data undergird every claim made in her new book.  细致的实地数据为她新书中的每一个论断打下了基础。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reinforce</td><td>/ˌriːɪnˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，强化</td><td>reinforce the message<br>强化这一信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated praise reinforced the child's interest in reading.  反复的表扬强化了孩子对阅读的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bear</td><td>/ˈber/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>承受，支撑；忍受</td><td>bear the weight<br>承受重量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These thin beams cannot bear the weight of the roof.  这些细梁承受不了屋顶的重量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bolster</td><td>/ˈboʊlstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，增强</td><td>bolster his confidence<br>增强他的信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A word of praise from her would bolster his confidence enormously.  她一句赞扬就会极大地增强他的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brace</td><td>/ˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使振作，鼓励；支撑</td><td>brace yourself for impact<br>做好承受冲击准备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cup of hot tea braced him for the long night.  一杯热茶让他为漫长的夜晚振作起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buttress</td><td>/ˈbʌtrɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，加强（论点）</td><td>buttress an argument<br>为论点提供支撑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh evidence buttressed the prosecutor's case against the two suspects.  新证据支持了检察官针对两名嫌疑人的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carry</td><td>/ˈkæri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支撑，承载</td><td>carry the weight<br>承载重量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Four stone pillars carry the whole weight of the roof.  四根石柱承载着整个屋顶的重量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stay</td><td>/ˈsteɪ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>支撑物，支柱；停留</td><td>a stay for the mast<br>桅杆的支索</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tent's stays snapped in the wind and everything collapsed.  帐篷的支索在风中断裂，一切都塌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sustain</td><td>/səˈsteɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支撑；维持</td><td>sustain economic growth<br>维持经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country cannot sustain such growth without more energy.  没有更多能源，这个国家无法维持这样的增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 underpin</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈpɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支撑，构成…的基础</td><td>underpin the whole theory<br>支撑整个理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful field data underpin every claim in this paper.  细致的实地数据支撑着本文的每一个论断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uphold</td><td>/ʌpˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，赞成；维持（原判）</td><td>uphold a decision<br>维持原判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeals court chose to uphold the original ruling in full.  上诉法院决定完全维持原判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prop up</td><td>/prɑːp ˈʌp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支撑，扶持</td><td>prop up the failing bank<br>扶持濒危银行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government spent billions to prop up the failing bank.  政府花了数十亿来扶持这家濒临倒闭的银行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shore up</td><td>/ʃɔr ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支撑，加固；支持</td><td>shore up public support<br>巩固公众支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government acted quickly to shore up the failing banks.  政府迅速采取行动支撑濒临倒闭的银行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undermine</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌmaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，暗中破坏</td><td>undermine confidence<br>削弱信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant rumors undermined confidence in the young manager.  不断的谣言削弱了人们对这位年轻经理的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weaken</td><td>/ˈwiːkən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，使变弱，变衰弱</td><td>weaken the structure<br>使结构变弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated flooding weakened the foundations of the stone bridge.  反复的洪水削弱了石桥的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>usher</b></td><td>/ˈʌʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开启</td><td>usher in a new era<br>开启新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invention of the printing press ushered in a new age of learning.  印刷机的发明开启了一个学术的新时代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 begin</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手</td><td>begin work on the project<br>开始着手这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We will begin the renovation as soon as the permit arrives.  许可一到我们就开始装修。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embark</td><td>/ɪmˈbɑːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>开始从事</td><td>embark on a new career<br>开始新的职业生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At forty she embarked on a new career as a literary translator.  四十岁时她开始从事文学翻译这一新职业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 start</td><td>/ˈstɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>from the start<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the start it was clear the schedule was too tight.  从一开始就明显看出日程太紧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commence</td><td>/kəˈmens/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手（正式）</td><td>commence legal proceedings<br>开始法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Construction will commence next month if funding is approved.  若资金获批，工程将于下月开始。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclude</td><td>/kənˈkluːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结束；得出结论</td><td>conclude the ceremony<br>结束仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge concluded that the evidence was too weak.  法官得出结论认为证据太薄弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 finish</td><td>/ˈfɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>完成，结束</td><td>finish the report tonight<br>今晚完成报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hopes to finish her thesis before the winter holiday.  她希望在寒假前完成论文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terminate</td><td>/ˈtɜːrməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>terminate the agreement<br>终止协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club terminated his membership without any explanation.  俱乐部未加解释就终止了他的会员资格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>underscore</b></td><td>/ˌʌndərˈskɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调</td><td>underscore the importance<br>强调重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report underscores the urgent need for cleaner drinking water.  这份报告强调了对更清洁饮用水的迫切需求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accentuate</td><td>/əkˈsentʃueɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调，突出</td><td>accentuate the difference<br>突出差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bright lighting accentuated every flaw in the painting.  明亮的灯光突显出这幅画的每处瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 italicize</td><td>/ɪˈtæləsaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用斜体强调</td><td>italicize the title<br>把标题排成斜体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please italicize every foreign word in the manuscript.  请把稿件中每个外来词排成斜体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stress</td><td>/ˈstres/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，着重</td><td>stress the importance<br>强调重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach stressed defense in every session this week.  本周每次训练教练都强调防守。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 underline</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌlaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调；在…下划线</td><td>underline the key point<br>强调关键点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The accident underlines the need for better street lighting.  这场事故凸显了改善街道照明的必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bring out</td><td>/brɪŋ ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使显现，突出</td><td>bring out the flavor<br>突出风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt brings out the sweetness of the tomatoes.  少许盐能突出西红柿的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 deemphasize</del></td><td>/diːˈemfəsaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不再强调，淡化</td><td>deemphasize test scores<br>淡化考试分数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school deemphasized rankings to reduce student anxiety.  学校淡化排名以减轻学生焦虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downplay</td><td>/ˌdaʊnˈpleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写，不予重视</td><td>downplay the significance<br>淡化其重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He downplayed his own role in the company's success.  他淡化了自己在公司成功中的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>understate</b></td><td>/ˌʌndərˈsteɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写；低估</td><td>understate the risks<br>低估风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials understated the flood damage to avoid alarming the public.  官员们对洪灾损失轻描淡写，以免引起公众恐慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downplay</td><td>/ˌdaʊnˈpleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写，不予重视</td><td>downplay the significance<br>淡化其重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He downplayed his own role in the company's success.  他淡化了自己在公司成功中的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conservative</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 low-key</td><td>/ˌloʊ ˈki/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>低调的，不张扬的</td><td>a low-key ceremony<br>一场低调的仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They chose a low-key wedding with only twelve guests.  他们选了只有十二位客人的低调婚礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muted</td><td>/ˈmjuːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>低调的，柔和的；压低的</td><td>a muted response<br>反应平淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The announcement drew a surprisingly muted response from investors.  这项公告得到投资者出奇平淡的反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repressed</td><td>/riˈprest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受压抑的，克制的</td><td>repressed anger<br>被压抑的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of repressed anger finally showed in his shaking voice.  多年压抑的怒气终于在他颤抖的声音里显露。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrained</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受约束的，克制的</td><td>restrained by rules<br>受规则约束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His restrained reply hid how angry he actually felt inside.  他克制的回答掩盖了他内心真正的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subdued</td><td>/səbˈduːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；柔和低沉的</td><td>in a subdued voice<br>用压低的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd grew subdued once the announcement was finally made.  消息宣布后，人群变得安静顺从。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unflashy</del></td><td>/ʌnˈflæʃi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不炫耀的，低调的</td><td>unflashy but effective<br>低调却有效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She favored unflashy clothes and quiet neighborhood restaurants.  她偏爱低调的衣着和安静的街边餐馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unpretentious</td><td>/ˌʌnprɪˈtenʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不炫耀的，朴实低调的</td><td>an unpretentious restaurant<br>朴实的小餐馆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They live in a small, unpretentious house near the river.  他们住在河边一间朴实的小房子里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 vaunt</del></td><td>/vɔːnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>吹嘘</td><td>vaunt one's achievements<br>夸耀自己的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He vaunted his prizes until even his friends stopped listening.  他不停吹嘘自己的奖项，连朋友都不再听了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exaggerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzædʒəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>夸大，夸张</td><td>exaggerate the danger<br>夸大危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tends to exaggerate how long the journey actually takes.  他总爱夸大这段路实际要走多久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overstate</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈsteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>夸大陈述，言过其实</td><td>overstate the benefits<br>夸大好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics say the report overstates the savings from the merger.  批评者说该报告夸大了合并带来的节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flamboyant</td><td>/flæmˈbɔɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>艳丽夺目的，炫耀的，充满装饰的</td><td>a flamboyant style<br>浮夸炫目的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The singer wore a flamboyant coat covered in mirrors and feathers.  歌手穿着一件缀满镜片和羽毛的艳丽夺目的外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flaring</td><td>/ˈflerɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耀眼张扬的；摇曳燃烧的</td><td>flaring colors<br>耀眼的色彩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer's flaring costume drew every eye in the theater.  舞者张扬耀眼的服装吸引了剧场里所有的目光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flashy</td><td>/ˈflæʃi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮华的，华而不实的</td><td>a flashy sports car<br>浮华的跑车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a flashy suit that everyone immediately noticed.  他穿着一身浮华的西装到场，所有人立刻都注意到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 garish</td><td>/ˈɡerɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于鲜艳的，过于张扬的</td><td>garish colors<br>过于鲜艳的颜色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lobby was painted in garish colors that hurt the eyes.  大堂被漆成刺眼的过于鲜艳的颜色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gaudy</td><td>/ˈɡɔːdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>俗丽的</td><td>gaudy jewelry<br>俗丽的首饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wore gaudy jewelry that clashed with her simple dress.  她戴着与朴素连衣裙不搭的俗丽首饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glitzy</td><td>/ˈɡlɪtsi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮华的，炫目的</td><td>a glitzy ceremony<br>浮华的典礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The glitzy launch party cost more than the product's research.  浮华的发布派对花费超过了产品研发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ostentatious</td><td>/ˌɑːstenˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫耀卖弄的</td><td>ostentatious display of wealth<br>炫富的排场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ostentatious diamond watch drew stares from everyone in the modest room.  他炫耀的钻石表引来了朴素房间里所有人的注视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 splashy</td><td>/ˈsplæʃi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人注目的，招眼的</td><td>a splashy advertisement<br>一则招眼的广告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine ran a splashy cover story about the singer.  杂志刊出一篇关于那位歌手的招眼封面报道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>undue</b></td><td>/ʌnˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的；不适当的</td><td>undue influence<br>不当的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge ruled that the will had been signed under undue influence.  法官裁定这份遗嘱是在不当影响下签署的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baroque</td><td>/bəˈroʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>装饰华丽的，过分雕琢的，复杂的</td><td>baroque ornamentation<br>华丽繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceiling was covered with heavy baroque ornamentation in gold.  天花板上覆满了华丽繁复的金色装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exorbitant</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrbɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的</td><td>exorbitant prices<br>过高的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourists complained about exorbitant prices for bottled water near the temple.  游客抱怨寺庙附近瓶装水价格过高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extravagant</td><td>/ɪkˈstrævəɡənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的；过度的，不必要的</td><td>extravagant spending<br>挥霍的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their extravagant spending on parties left the charity nearly broke.  他们在宴会上的挥霍开支让这家慈善机构几乎破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extreme</td><td>/ɪkˈstriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>极端的，极度的；极端</td><td>extreme views<br>极端的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His extreme views cost him most of his old friends.  他极端的观点让他失去了大部分老朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immoderate</td><td>/ɪˈmɑdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无节制的，过度的</td><td>immoderate drinking<br>无节制的饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her immoderate praise made the young writer distinctly uneasy.  她过度的赞美让这位年轻作家明显不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excessive</td><td>/ɪkˈsesɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的</td><td>excessive drinking<br>过量饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors warned him about his excessive salt intake.  医生警告他盐摄入过量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 middling</td><td>/ˈmɪdlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>中等的，一般的</td><td>middling quality<br>中等的质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restaurant serves middling food at prices that feel a bit high.  这家餐厅食物一般，价格却偏高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>unexceptionable</b></del></td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈsepʃənəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可挑剔的，无可指责的</td><td>unexceptionable manners<br>无可挑剔的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her conduct throughout the inquiry was unexceptionable.  在整个调查中她的行为无可指责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unassailable</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈseɪləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的，无法否认的，不可亵渎的</td><td>an unassailable position<br>无法攻破的地位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Decades of careful research gave her an unassailable reputation among historians.  数十年的严谨研究使她在史学界享有无可争辩的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undeniable</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈnaɪəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可否认的，明显的</td><td>undeniable talent<br>不可否认的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her influence on modern dance is undeniable.  她对现代舞的影响不可否认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irrefutable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈfjuːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可反驳的</td><td>irrefutable evidence<br>无可反驳的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letters gave irrefutable proof of his involvement.  这些信件提供了他参与其中的铁证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indisputable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的</td><td>indisputable evidence<br>无可争辩的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs offered indisputable proof of the damage.  这些照片提供了损害的无可争辩的证明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unimpeachable</td><td>/ˌʌnɪmˈpiːtʃəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可怀疑的，无懈可击的</td><td>unimpeachable integrity<br>无可置疑的正直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had an unimpeachable record of public service.  这位证人有无可指责的公职记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 assailable</del></td><td>/əˈseɪləbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受攻击的，站不住脚的</td><td>an assailable claim<br>站不住脚的主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument proved assailable on almost every point.  他的论证几乎每一点都站不住脚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 controversial</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，引起争论的</td><td>a controversial decision<br>有争议的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's controversial ruling was debated for months afterward.  法官那项有争议的裁决之后被讨论了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unflappable</b></td><td>/ˌʌnˈflæpəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，从容不迫的</td><td>an unflappable leader<br>沉着的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even during the emergency landing the pilot remained completely unflappable.  即使在紧急降落时，飞行员依然十分镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collected</td><td>/kəˈlektəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，冷静的</td><td>calm and collected<br>冷静镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed cool and collected throughout the tense negotiation.  整个紧张的谈判过程中她都保持冷静镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 composed</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇静的，沉着的</td><td>remain composed<br>保持镇静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed composed while answering the reporters' hostile questions.  她在回答记者敌意的提问时始终保持镇静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disimpassioned</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪmˈpæʃənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的，不激动的</td><td>a disimpassioned observer<br>冷静的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a disimpassioned account of the violent quarrel.  她冷静地叙述了那场激烈的争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperturbable</td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈtɜːrbəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的；不慌不忙的</td><td>imperturbable calm<br>泰然自若的镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot stayed imperturbable while the engine warning sounded.  发动机警报响起时，飞行员依然沉着冷静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 perturbable</del></td><td>/pərˈtɜrbəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易被扰乱的，易慌乱的</td><td>easily perturbable<br>很容易慌乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A perturbable pilot would never survive such a storm.  易慌乱的飞行员绝无法在这种风暴中生还。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 shakable</del></td><td>/ˈʃeɪkəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可动摇的，不坚定的</td><td>a shakable belief<br>可被动摇的信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their loyalty turned out to be quite shakable under pressure.  压力之下他们的忠诚原来很容易动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturbed</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrbd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不安的，受扰的</td><td>deeply disturbed by news<br>被消息深深扰乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was disturbed by the noise from the next room.  他被隔壁的噪音扰得不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nervous</td><td>/ˈnɜːrvəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧张不安的，忧虑的</td><td>nervous about the results<br>对结果感到不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew nervous about the results as the week passed.  一周过去，她对结果越来越不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ungainly</b></td><td>/ʌnˈɡeɪnli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不雅的</td><td>an ungainly walk<br>笨拙的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young bird made an ungainly landing on the narrow branch.  那只幼鸟笨拙地落在窄窄的树枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clumsy</td><td>/ˈklʌmzi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的</td><td>a clumsy attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My clumsy attempt at juggling ended with three broken plates.  我笨拙地学抛球，结果打碎了三个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awkward</td><td>/ˈɔːkwərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的；尴尬的</td><td>an awkward silence<br>尴尬的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awkward silence followed his remark about her divorce.  他谈到她离婚之后，出现了一阵尴尬的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blundering</td><td>/ˈblʌndərɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨手笨脚的，出错百出的</td><td>a blundering attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His blundering repair left the pipe worse than before.  他笨拙的修理让水管比原来更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maladroit</td><td>/ˌmæləˈdrɔɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的</td><td>a maladroit attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His maladroit attempt at an apology only made the situation worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试只是让情况变得更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coordinated</td><td>/koʊˈɔːrdəneɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>协调的，动作灵活的</td><td>well coordinated movements<br>协调的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gymnasts must be far more coordinated than most athletes.  体操运动员必须比多数运动员协调得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 graceful</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>优雅的，优美的</td><td>a graceful dancer<br>姿态优雅的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a graceful turn and landed without a sound.  她优雅地一个转身，落地时毫无声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adroit</td><td>/əˈdrɔɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，灵巧的</td><td>adroit at negotiation<br>擅长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She proved remarkably adroit at handling hostile questions from reporters.  她在应对记者的敌意提问时表现得非常娴熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dexterous</td><td>/ˈdekstrəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>动作灵活的；头脑灵活的</td><td>dexterous fingers<br>灵巧的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon's dexterous hands worked steadily for more than six hours.  这位外科医生灵活的双手稳稳地工作了六个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 lissome</del></td><td>/ˈlɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的；敏捷的，轻盈的</td><td>a lissome dancer<br>身姿柔软的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lissome dancer moved across the stage as if she were weightless.  那位身姿柔软的舞者仿佛毫无重量地掠过舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unimpeachable</b></td><td>/ˌʌnɪmˈpiːtʃəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可怀疑的，无懈可击的</td><td>unimpeachable integrity<br>无可置疑的正直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had an unimpeachable record of public service.  这位证人有无可指责的公职记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unassailable</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈseɪləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的，无法否认的，不可亵渎的</td><td>an unassailable position<br>无法攻破的地位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Decades of careful research gave her an unassailable reputation among historians.  数十年的严谨研究使她在史学界享有无可争辩的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undeniable</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈnaɪəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可否认的，明显的</td><td>undeniable talent<br>不可否认的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her influence on modern dance is undeniable.  她对现代舞的影响不可否认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irrefutable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈfjuːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可反驳的</td><td>irrefutable evidence<br>无可反驳的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letters gave irrefutable proof of his involvement.  这些信件提供了他参与其中的铁证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indisputable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的</td><td>indisputable evidence<br>无可争辩的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs offered indisputable proof of the damage.  这些照片提供了损害的无可争辩的证明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unexceptionable</del></td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈsepʃənəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可挑剔的，无可指责的</td><td>unexceptionable manners<br>无可挑剔的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her conduct throughout the inquiry was unexceptionable.  在整个调查中她的行为无可指责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 assailable</del></td><td>/əˈseɪləbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受攻击的，站不住脚的</td><td>an assailable claim<br>站不住脚的主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument proved assailable on almost every point.  他的论证几乎每一点都站不住脚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 controversial</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，引起争论的</td><td>a controversial decision<br>有争议的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's controversial ruling was debated for months afterward.  法官那项有争议的裁决之后被讨论了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open to question</td><td>/ˈoʊpən tu ˈkwestʃən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有疑问的，可质疑的</td><td>still open to question<br>仍有疑问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether the drug works at all remains open to question.  这种药是否有效仍有疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unkempt</b></td><td>/ˌʌnˈkempt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不整洁的，蓬乱的</td><td>unkempt hair<br>蓬乱的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived at the interview with unkempt hair and a wrinkled shirt.  他去面试时头发蓬乱，衬衫也皱皱巴巴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slovenly</td><td>/ˈslʌvnli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>邋遢的，不整洁的</td><td>slovenly appearance<br>邋遢的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His slovenly desk made a bad impression on every visitor.  他邋遢的办公桌给每位来访者留下坏印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slipshod</td><td>/ˈslɪpʃɑːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，随意的</td><td>slipshod workmanship<br>马虎的做工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inspector criticized the slipshod workmanship on the newly built bridge.  检查员批评新建桥梁马虎的做工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sloppy</td><td>/ˈslɑːpi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>邋遢的，不整洁的；混乱的</td><td>sloppy handwriting<br>潦草的字迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sloppy handwriting made the exam answers almost impossible to read.  他潦草的字迹让考卷答案几乎无法辨认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unneat</del></td><td>/ʌnˈnit/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不整洁的，凌乱的</td><td>an unneat desk<br>凌乱的书桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unneat handwriting cost him marks on every paper.  他不整洁的字迹让他每份考卷都扣分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untidy</td><td>/ʌnˈtaɪdi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不整洁的，凌乱的</td><td>an untidy desk<br>凌乱的书桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His untidy desk was buried under unopened letters.  他凌乱的书桌被未开封的信件埋住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 topsy-turvy</td><td>/ˌtɑːpsiˈtɜːrvi/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乱七八糟的，颠倒的</td><td>a topsy-turvy room<br>乱七八糟的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movers left the whole apartment topsy-turvy.  搬运工把整套公寓弄得乱七八糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Jumbled</td><td>/ˈdʒʌmbəld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>混乱的，杂乱的</td><td>a jumbled pile of clothes<br>杂乱的一堆衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His notes were so jumbled that nobody could follow them.  他的笔记杂乱得没人看得懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bandbox</td><td>/ˈbændbɑːks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>光鲜整洁的；帽盒</td><td>a bandbox appearance<br>光鲜整洁的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always looked as if he had stepped out of a bandbox.  他总是一身光鲜整洁的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 neat</td><td>/ˈniːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>整洁的，井然有序的</td><td>neat handwriting<br>整洁的字迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her neat handwriting made the notes easy to read.  她整洁的字迹让笔记很好读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 orderly</td><td>/ˈɔːrdərli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有秩序的，整齐的</td><td>an orderly queue<br>整齐的队伍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students left the hall in an orderly line.  学生们排成整齐的队伍离开礼堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 organized</td><td>/ˈɔːrɡəˌnaɪzd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有条理的，有组织的</td><td>highly organized files<br>条理分明的文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She keeps an organized notebook for every course she takes.  她为每门课都备有一本条理分明的笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shipshape</td><td>/ˈʃɪpʃeɪp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>井然有序的</td><td>keep the office shipshape<br>让办公室井然有序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old sailor kept his tiny cabin perfectly shipshape at all times.  老水手始终把他的小舱室保持得井然有序。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tidy</td><td>/ˈtaɪdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>整洁的；整理</td><td>tidy up the room<br>把房间整理干净</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please tidy up the room before the guests arrive.  请在客人到来前把房间整理干净。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trim</td><td>/ˈtrɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>修剪；削减</td><td>trim the hedge<br>修剪树篱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He trims the hedge every June before the neighbors complain.  他每年六月在邻居抱怨前修剪树篱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncluttered</td><td>/ʌnˈklʌtərd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>整齐不杂乱的</td><td>an uncluttered desk<br>不杂乱的书桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She works best at an uncluttered table.  她在不杂乱的桌前工作效率最高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-ordered</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈɔrdərd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>秩序井然的，有条理的</td><td>a well-ordered household<br>井井有条的家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She kept a well-ordered household despite four small children.  尽管有四个小孩，她仍把家管理得井井有条。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unlettered</b></td><td>/ˌʌnˈletərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受教育的，文盲的</td><td>unlettered villagers<br>未受教育的村民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many unlettered farmers memorized long songs instead of writing them down.  许多未受教育的农民靠记诵长歌，而不是把它们写下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ignorant</td><td>/ˈɪɡnərənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知的，不知道的</td><td>ignorant of the facts<br>不了解事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were completely ignorant of the danger ahead.  他们对前方的危险毫不知情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illiterate</td><td>/ɪˈlɪtərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>文盲的，不识字的</td><td>functionally illiterate adults<br>实际上文盲的成人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Millions remain illiterate because schools in their villages closed years ago.  数百万人仍是文盲，因为他们村里的学校多年前就关了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uneducated</td><td>/ʌnˈedʒukeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受教育的，无知的</td><td>largely uneducated farm workers<br>大多未受教育的农场工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Though uneducated, he could repair any engine brought to his shop.  他虽未受过教育，却能修好送到店里的任何发动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untutored</td><td>/ʌnˈtutərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受教育的，未经训练的</td><td>an untutored eye<br>未经训练的眼光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even to an untutored ear the violin sounded out of tune.  即便没受训练的耳朵也听出小提琴走调了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 educated</td><td>/ˈedʒəˌkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受过教育的，有学识的</td><td>a well educated reader<br>受过良好教育的读者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An educated reader would notice the error in the very first paragraph.  受过良好教育的读者会在第一段就发现这个错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 knowledgeable</td><td>/ˈnɑːlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>知识丰富的，了解很多的</td><td>knowledgeable about wine<br>懂葡萄酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our guide was remarkably knowledgeable about local birds.  我们的导游对当地鸟类了解得非常多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 erudite</td><td>/ˈerudaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>博学的</td><td>an erudite scholar<br>博学的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The erudite professor could quote ancient poetry from memory for hours.  那位博学的教授能凭记忆连续几小时引用古诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 literate</td><td>/ˈlɪtərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有读写能力的；有学识的</td><td>a literate population<br>有文化的人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Barely half the adults in that region were literate a century ago.  一个世纪前该地区成年人中识字的勉强过半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 schooled</td><td>/ˈskuːld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受过训练的，有素养的</td><td>schooled in classics<br>受过古典学训练</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was schooled in law long before entering politics.  她在从政之前很久就接受过法律训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-informed</td><td>/ˌwel ɪnˈfɔrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见闻广博的，消息灵通的</td><td>a well-informed source<br>消息可靠的来源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A well-informed reader spotted the error in the report.  一位见闻广博的读者发现了报告中的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-read</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈrɛd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>博览群书的，博学的</td><td>a well-read editor<br>博览群书的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is well-read enough to place any quotation within seconds.  她博览群书，能几秒内说出任何引文的出处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unpretentious</b></td><td>/ˌʌnprɪˈtenʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不炫耀的，朴实低调的</td><td>an unpretentious restaurant<br>朴实的小餐馆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They live in a small, unpretentious house near the river.  他们住在河边一间朴实的小房子里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demure</td><td>/dɪˈmjʊr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>端庄的，娴静的</td><td>a demure smile<br>娴静的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a demure nod and said nothing more.  她端庄地点了点头，没再多说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 down-to-earth</td><td>/ˌdaʊn tu ˈɜrθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>务实的，不摆架子的</td><td>a down-to-earth manner<br>务实的作风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite his fame he remained a down-to-earth neighbor.  尽管成名，他仍是个不摆架子的邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lowly</td><td>/ˈloʊli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>地位低的，卑微的</td><td>a lowly clerk<br>一个卑微的职员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He began as a lowly assistant and rose to direct the firm.  他从卑微的助理做起，后来掌管了公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meek</td><td>/miːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a meek and quiet manner<br>温顺安静的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too meek to complain, he accepted the extra work without saying a word.  他太顺从了，不敢抱怨，一句话没说就接下了额外的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unassuming</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈsuːmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦逊的，不张扬的</td><td>quiet and unassuming<br>安静谦逊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The famous author was surprisingly unassuming in person.  这位著名作家本人出乎意料地谦逊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unostentatious</del></td><td>/ˌʌnɑstenˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不炫耀的，低调的</td><td>unostentatious wealth<br>不外露的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led an unostentatious life despite owning three factories.  尽管拥有三家工厂，他生活十分低调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artless</td><td>/ˈɑrtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏技巧的，笨拙的</td><td>an artless imitation<br>拙劣的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting was an artless copy of a famous portrait.  这幅画是对一幅名作的拙劣临摹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genuine</td><td>/ˈdʒenjəwən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真正的，真实的；真诚的</td><td>genuine leather<br>真皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts confirmed that the signature on the letter was genuine.  专家确认信上的签名是真的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honest</td><td>/ˈɑːnəst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诚实的，正直的</td><td>an honest answer<br>一个诚实的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me an honest answer, even if it disappoints me.  给我一个诚实的答复，即使会让我失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ingenuous</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒenjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天真淳朴的</td><td>an ingenuous young student<br>天真淳朴的年轻学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ingenuous questions about the city amused the veteran taxi driver greatly.  他关于这座城市的天真提问让老出租车司机大为好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innocent</td><td>/ˈɪnəsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，无恶意的；无辜的</td><td>an innocent remark<br>无恶意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What began as an innocent joke ended in a serious quarrel.  一句本无恶意的玩笑最后引发了激烈争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 naive</td><td>/naɪˈiːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天真的，幼稚的</td><td>naive assumptions<br>天真的假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was naive to expect honesty from someone with that record.  指望有那种记录的人诚实太天真了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sincere</td><td>/sɪnˈsɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真诚的，诚挚的</td><td>a sincere apology<br>真诚的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sincere apology repaired a friendship damaged for years.  她真诚的道歉修复了受损多年的友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 true</td><td>/ˈtruː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a true story<br>一个真实的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a true story from wartime.  这部电影取材于战时的一个真实故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unpretending</del></td><td>/ˌʌnprɪˈtendɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不做作的，朴实的</td><td>an unpretending manner<br>朴实无华的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite his fame, he kept an unpretending and quiet lifestyle.  尽管成名，他仍保持朴实安静的生活方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arrogant</td><td>/ˈærəɡənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，自大的</td><td>an arrogant manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arrogant remarks cost him the respect of his colleagues.  他傲慢的言论使他失去了同事的尊重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bumptious</td><td>/ˈbʌmpʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横傲慢的，自以为是的</td><td>a bumptious young man<br>自以为是的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her bumptious manner annoyed the older members of the committee.  她自以为是的态度让委员会里年长的成员很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conceited</td><td>/kənˈsiːtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自负的，骄傲自满的</td><td>a conceited young actor<br>自负的年轻演员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew conceited after winning a single small prize.  他只赢了一个小奖就变得自负起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 egotistic</td><td>/ˌiːɡəˈtɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我中心的，自负的</td><td>an egotistic remark<br>自负的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director was too egotistic to accept advice from anyone.  这位主管太自负，不肯接受任何人的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 fastuous</del></td><td>/ˈfæstʃuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的；浮夸的</td><td>a fastuous manner<br>傲慢浮夸的姿态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fastuous display of learning bored the other guests.  他浮夸地炫耀学问，让其他客人厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 haughty</td><td>/ˈhɔːti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，目中无人的</td><td>a haughty manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the waiter a haughty look and said nothing.  她傲慢地看了服务员一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imperious</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>an imperious tone<br>专横的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her imperious manner made junior colleagues afraid to disagree.  她专横的态度让年轻同事不敢有异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lordly</td><td>/ˈlɔːrdli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的；高贵的</td><td>a lordly manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waved the waiter away with a lordly gesture.  他以傲慢的手势把侍者挥退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overweening</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈwiːnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>overweening pride<br>过度的骄傲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His overweening confidence led him to ignore every warning from the engineers.  他过度的自信使他忽视了工程师的每一次警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peremptory</td><td>/pəˈremptəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的；专横傲慢的</td><td>a peremptory order<br>不容置疑的命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His peremptory tone left no room for questions or discussion.  他傲慢的语气不给任何提问或讨论的余地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pompous</td><td>/ˈpɑːmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮夸的；傲慢的</td><td>a pompous speech<br>浮夸的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pompous speech bored everyone in the crowded town hall.  那场浮夸的演讲让拥挤的市政厅里每个人都厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 presuming</td><td>/prɪˈzuːmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自以为是的，冒失放肆的</td><td>a presuming stranger<br>冒失的陌生人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was presuming of him to offer advice uninvited.  他未经邀请就给建议，实在冒失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 presumptuous</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmptʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冒失的，放肆的</td><td>presumptuous of you<br>你太放肆了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems presumptuous to criticize a book you have not read.  批评一本没读过的书，似乎太放肆了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-asserting</td><td>/ˌself əˈsɜːrtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我主张强的，好出头的</td><td>a self-asserting personality<br>好出头的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The self-asserting newcomer soon dominated every meeting.  这位好出头的新人很快主导了每次会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 supercilious</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsɪliəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高傲的，傲慢的</td><td>a supercilious smile<br>傲慢的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supercilious smile crossed his face when he heard my question.  听到我的问题，他脸上掠过傲慢的微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 superior</td><td>/suːˈpɪriər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>更好的，占优势的；上级</td><td>superior to the rest<br>优于其余各方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This model is clearly superior to the older version.  这个型号明显优于旧版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 toplofty</del></td><td>/ˈtɑpˌlɔfti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自大的，趾高气扬的</td><td>a toplofty attitude<br>趾高气扬的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His toplofty remarks about the village offended everyone there.  他对那个村子趾高气扬的评论得罪了当地所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uppish</del></td><td>/ˈʌpɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自命不凡的，傲慢的</td><td>an uppish clerk<br>傲慢的店员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The uppish clerk barely looked at the waiting customers.  那个傲慢的店员几乎不看等候的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flamboyant</td><td>/flæmˈbɔɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>艳丽夺目的，炫耀的，充满装饰的</td><td>a flamboyant style<br>浮夸炫目的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The singer wore a flamboyant coat covered in mirrors and feathers.  歌手穿着一件缀满镜片和羽毛的艳丽夺目的外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ostentatious</td><td>/ˌɑːstenˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫耀卖弄的</td><td>ostentatious display of wealth<br>炫富的排场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ostentatious diamond watch drew stares from everyone in the modest room.  他炫耀的钻石表引来了朴素房间里所有人的注视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affected</td><td>/əˈfektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>装出来的，做作的</td><td>an affected accent<br>装出来的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke with an affected accent that fooled nobody present.  她用装出来的口音说话，没骗过在场任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artful</td><td>/ˈɑːrtfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺高超的；狡猾的</td><td>an artful arrangement<br>巧妙的布置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef's artful plating made the dish look like a painting.  厨师巧妙的摆盘让这道菜看起来像幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artificial</td><td>/ˌɑːrtəˈfɪʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>人造的；虚假的</td><td>artificial flowers<br>人造花</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lobby was decorated with artificial flowers that never wilted.  大厅用永不枯萎的人造花装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuming</td><td>/əˈsuːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自以为是的，傲慢的</td><td>an assuming young man<br>自以为是的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The assuming clerk corrected the professor's own citation.  那位自以为是的职员纠正了教授本人的引注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissembling</td><td>/dɪˈsemblɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>掩饰真情，假装</td><td>tired of his dissembling<br>厌倦他的掩饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months of dissembling he finally admitted the truth.  掩饰了数月之后，他终于承认了真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dissimulating</del></td><td>/dɪˈsɪmjəleɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>掩饰的，虚伪的</td><td>a dissimulating smile<br>掩饰真心的笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her dissimulating calm hid real panic about the results.  她掩饰出的镇静藏住了对结果的真正恐慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fake</td><td>/ˈfeɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>假的，伪造的；伪造品</td><td>a fake diploma<br>假文凭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery unknowingly displayed a fake for almost ten years.  这家画廊不知情地展出了一件赝品近十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 guileful</del></td><td>/ˈɡaɪlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡诈的，欺骗的</td><td>a guileful negotiator<br>狡诈的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guileful merchant tricked several villagers out of their savings.  这个狡诈的商人骗走了几位村民的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insincere</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪnˈsɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不诚实的，虚伪的</td><td>an insincere apology<br>虚伪的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insincere apology only made the situation considerably worse.  他虚伪的道歉只让情况更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 phony</td><td>/ˈfoʊni/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>假的，冒充的；骗子</td><td>a phony accent<br>假装的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a phony name when renting the small apartment.  他租那间小公寓时用了假名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unflagging</b></td><td>/ˌʌnˈflæɡɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不懈的；不衰退的</td><td>unflagging enthusiasm<br>不减的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unflagging enthusiasm kept the whole team going for months.  她不懈的热情支撑着整个团队坚持了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indefatigable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈfætɪɡəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不倦的，不屈不挠的</td><td>an indefatigable worker<br>不知疲倦的工作者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was an indefatigable campaigner for cleaner rivers.  她是为河流洁净不懈奔走的活动家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inexhaustible</td><td>/ˌɪnɪɡˈzɔːstəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无穷的，不会枯竭的</td><td>inexhaustible energy<br>无穷的精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children seemed to have an inexhaustible supply of questions.  孩子们的问题似乎无穷无尽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tireless</td><td>/ˈtaɪərləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不疲倦的，不懈的</td><td>a tireless campaigner<br>不懈的活动家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a tireless campaigner for clean water.  她是一位不懈争取清洁饮水的活动家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrelenting</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不松懈的，不屈不挠的</td><td>unrelenting pressure<br>持续不断的压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unrelenting effort finally forced the company to apologize.  她不懈的努力最终迫使公司道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 weariless</del></td><td>/ˈwɪriləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不疲倦的，不厌倦的</td><td>weariless devotion<br>不倦的投入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His weariless devotion to the project impressed everyone.  他对这个项目不倦的投入让所有人钦佩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lackadaisical</td><td>/ˌlækəˈdeɪzɪkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒散的，无兴趣的</td><td>a lackadaisical attitude<br>懒散的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lackadaisical attitude toward homework worried both parents.  他对作业懒散的态度让父母都很担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 languid</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A languid summer afternoon passed with nobody willing to leave the shade.  一个慢悠悠的夏日午后过去了，没人愿意离开树荫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lethargic</td><td>/ləˈθɑːrdʒɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，行动迟缓的</td><td>feel lethargic all day<br>整天没精打采</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left everyone lethargic and unwilling to move before evening.  酷热让所有人都没精打采，天黑前都不愿动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 listless</td><td>/ˈlɪstləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无精打采的，倦怠的</td><td>feel listless and tired<br>感到倦怠疲乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever left her listless for nearly a week.  发烧让她将近一周都无精打采。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sluggish</td><td>/ˈslʌɡɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>行动迟缓的，懒散的</td><td>feel sluggish all morning<br>整个上午都懒洋洋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt sluggish all morning and could barely finish his simplest chores.  他整个上午都懒洋洋的，连最简单的杂事都几乎做不完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torpid</td><td>/ˈtɔːrpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的，无生气的</td><td>a torpid winter afternoon<br>慵懒迟滞的冬日下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heat left the whole village torpid and unwilling to move.  酷热让整个村子都变得麻木迟钝，不愿动弹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unsung</b></td><td>/ˌʌnˈsʌŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被埋没的；不知名的</td><td>an unsung hero<br>无名英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unsung engineers behind the project never appeared in any press photo.  项目背后那些默默无闻的工程师从未出现在任何新闻照片里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 noteless</del></td><td>/ˈnoʊtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不出名的，无人注意的</td><td>a noteless village<br>无名的村庄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was born in a noteless village near the border.  他生在边境附近一个无名村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uncelebrated</del></td><td>/ʌnˈsɛlɪbreɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受赞誉的，不出名的</td><td>an uncelebrated pioneer<br>未受赞誉的先驱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains an uncelebrated pioneer of early radio.  她仍是早期广播一位未受赞誉的先驱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unfamous</del></td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪməs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不出名的</td><td>an unfamous painter<br>不出名的画家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery deliberately shows work by unfamous painters.  这家画廊有意展出不出名画家的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unknown</td><td>/ˌʌnˈnoʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未知的，不为人知的</td><td>an unknown author<br>无名的作者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diary was written by an unknown author in 1830.  这本日记由一位无名作者于1830年写成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrecognized</td><td>/ˌʌnˈrekəɡnaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被认可的，未被认出的</td><td>unrecognized talent<br>未被认可的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His talent went unrecognized until long after his death.  他的才华直到死后很久才被认可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 celebrated</td><td>/ˈseləˌbreɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，受赞誉的</td><td>a celebrated stage actor<br>著名的舞台演员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays work by several celebrated local painters.  博物馆展出了几位著名本地画家的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 famed</td><td>/ˈfeɪmd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闻名的，著名的</td><td>famed for her courage<br>以勇气闻名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley is famed for its cold, clear spring water.  这座山谷以清冷的泉水而闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 famous</td><td>/ˈfeɪməs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，出名的</td><td>famous for its seafood<br>以海鲜闻名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town is famous for its handmade paper.  这座小镇以手工纸而闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noted</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，知名的</td><td>a noted scholar<br>知名学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A noted scholar of Persian poetry opened the conference.  一位知名的波斯诗歌学者为会议开幕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prominent</td><td>/ˈprɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的；显眼的</td><td>a prominent local politician<br>当地知名的政治人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name appears in a prominent place on the donor list.  她的名字出现在捐赠名单的显眼位置。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 renowned</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有名望的，著名的</td><td>renowned throughout Europe<br>闻名整个欧洲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city is renowned for its ancient bridges and narrow lanes.  这座城市以古桥和窄巷闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-known</td><td>/ˌwelˈnoʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>众所周知的，著名的</td><td>a well-known fact<br>众所周知的事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is a well-known fact that the bridge leaks.  这座桥漏水是众所周知的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>unruly</b></td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 indocile</del></td><td>/ɪnˈdɑsəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听教的，难驯服的</td><td>an indocile pupil<br>不听教的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The indocile colt refused every rider that season.  那匹难驯的小马那一季拒绝了所有骑手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indomitable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈdɑːmətəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不屈不挠的，不可征服的</td><td>indomitable spirit<br>不屈的精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her indomitable will carried the team through the final round.  她不屈的意志带全队走完了最后一轮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncontrollable</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈtroʊləbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法控制的</td><td>uncontrollable laughter<br>无法控制的大笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fire became uncontrollable once it reached the dry pines.  火势一到干松林就无法控制了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undisciplined</td><td>/ʌnˈdɪsəplɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不守纪律的，散漫的</td><td>an undisciplined class<br>散漫的班级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The undisciplined squad lost every match by wide margins.  这支散漫的队伍每场都大比分落败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ungovernable</td><td>/ʌnˈɡʌvərnəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以控制的，无法管束的</td><td>an ungovernable temper<br>无法控制的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd became ungovernable once the gates were opened.  大门一开，人群就变得无法控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unmanageable</td><td>/ənˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以管理的，难控制的</td><td>unmanageable debt<br>难以控制的债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class grew unmanageable after the substitute arrived.  代课老师来后，这个班变得难以管理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 biddable</td><td>/ˈbɪdəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，听话的</td><td>a biddable pupil<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biddable pupil did every task without complaint.  那听话的学生毫无抱怨地完成每项任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conformable</td><td>/kənˈfɔrməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；一致的</td><td>conformable to the rules<br>符合规定的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 subdued</td><td>/səbˈduːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；柔和低沉的</td><td>in a subdued voice<br>用压低的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd grew subdued once the announcement was finally made.  消息宣布后，人群变得安静顺从。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mild</td><td>/ˈmaɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，不严厉的</td><td>a mild punishment<br>轻微的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winter here is unusually mild and rarely brings snow.  这里的冬天格外温和，很少下雪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 manageable</td><td>/ˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易管理的，好对付的</td><td>a manageable workload<br>可应付的工作量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Split the task into smaller, manageable pieces first.  先把任务拆成较小、好处理的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disciplined</td><td>/ˈdɪsəplənd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守纪律的，自律的</td><td>a disciplined class<br>守纪律的班级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her disciplined class worked quietly without any supervision.  她那守纪律的班级无人监督也安静做事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>untenable</b></td><td>/ʌnˈtenəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>防守不住的，站不住脚的</td><td>an untenable position<br>站不住脚的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Once the new evidence appeared, his earlier theory became completely untenable.  新证据出现后，他先前的理论就完全站不住脚了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 assailable</del></td><td>/əˈseɪləbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易受攻击的，站不住脚的</td><td>an assailable claim<br>站不住脚的主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument proved assailable on almost every point.  他的论证几乎每一点都站不住脚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 controversial</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，引起争论的</td><td>a controversial decision<br>有争议的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's controversial ruling was debated for months afterward.  法官那项有争议的裁决之后被讨论了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 open to question</td><td>/ˈoʊpən tu ˈkwestʃən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有疑问的，可质疑的</td><td>still open to question<br>仍有疑问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether the drug works at all remains open to question.  这种药是否有效仍有疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unassailable</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈseɪləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的，无法否认的，不可亵渎的</td><td>an unassailable position<br>无法攻破的地位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Decades of careful research gave her an unassailable reputation among historians.  数十年的严谨研究使她在史学界享有无可争辩的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undeniable</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈnaɪəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可否认的，明显的</td><td>undeniable talent<br>不可否认的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her influence on modern dance is undeniable.  她对现代舞的影响不可否认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irrefutable</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈfjuːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可反驳的</td><td>irrefutable evidence<br>无可反驳的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letters gave irrefutable proof of his involvement.  这些信件提供了他参与其中的铁证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indisputable</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可争辩的</td><td>indisputable evidence<br>无可争辩的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs offered indisputable proof of the damage.  这些照片提供了损害的无可争辩的证明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unexceptionable</del></td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈsepʃənəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可挑剔的，无可指责的</td><td>unexceptionable manners<br>无可挑剔的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her conduct throughout the inquiry was unexceptionable.  在整个调查中她的行为无可指责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unimpeachable</td><td>/ˌʌnɪmˈpiːtʃəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可怀疑的，无懈可击的</td><td>unimpeachable integrity<br>无可置疑的正直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had an unimpeachable record of public service.  这位证人有无可指责的公职记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>untold</b></td><td>/ʌnˈtoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>数不清的，无数的</td><td>untold wealth<br>数不清的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shipwreck was said to carry untold wealth in gold.  据说那艘沉船载有数不清的黄金财富。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incalculable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈkælkjələbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>数不清的，无法估量的</td><td>incalculable damage<br>无法估量的损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flood did incalculable damage to the old town center.  洪水给老城中心造成无法估量的损失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innumerable</td><td>/ɪˈnuːmərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无数的，数不清的</td><td>innumerable stars<br>无数的星星</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Innumerable stars appeared once the city lights went out.  城灯一灭，无数星星就出现了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 countless</td><td>/ˈkaʊntləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无数的，数不清的</td><td>countless attempts<br>无数次尝试</td></tr>
